A trainable output scale removes most of the resulting accuracy differences, leaving AD ahead of its twirls by at most about three percentage points in our simulations; what it removes reappears as a cost in measurement shots: trained and tested with $10^3$ shots per image, a four-qubit classifier under AD at $p=0.3$ stays within 1.5 points of noiseless accuracy, while the twirled classifiers lose up to 33. At weaker damping the separation depth grows roughly as $(np)^{-1}\ln(1/p)$. The damping direction is a gauge when the damping follows complete entangling layers and the circuit boundaries are trainable; in an eigensolver it becomes physical inside a decomposed two-qubit gate.

Abstract: Traditional discrete randomized compiling (RC) tailors errors with random control frames at logical-gate boundaries, but fails to capture intra-instrument control evolution or persistent system-environment coupling. In memory-bearing environments, errors correlate across times, not just single gates. We build continuous-process randomized compilation (CPRC) in the continuous process tensor (cPT) framework, unifying random unitary control trajectories, pointwise-covariant finite-duration instruments, and environmental dynamics on one time axis. We study four protocols: fixed Pauli refresh, bounded Cayley paths, unitary Brownian motion, Ornstein-Uhlenbeck driving. We obtain three key results: (1) Pointwise covariance ensures each trajectory exactly reproduces the target logic without system-environment coupling; a closed-frame counterexample proves endpoint-only compensation causes first-order instrument errors. (2) Independent Pauli conjugations over full intervals diagonalize error histories at boundaries, but classical labels retain environment-induced correlations, confirmed by an exact static-bath solution. (3) We derive a total-variation error bound for full adaptive output records under bounded centered coupling and mixing conditions. We prove convergence of trajectory-averaged cPT coefficients in fixed particle sectors for finite non-Gaussian environments and Gaussian baths with time-integrable covariance, including the uncut Drude spectrum. We numerically validate the theory in a qubit model via output probabilities, operator conditional mutual information, and all two-particle coefficients for both qubit and thermal Drude baths. This work extends RC from discrete circuits to continuous-time memory-bearing quantum processes, offering a unified framework, quantitative tools, implementable schemes, and benchmarks for non-Markovian error tailoring.

</details>

Abstract: Learning quantum interactions from finite-temperature many-body systems is a central task in emerging quantum platforms. Recently, the problem of learning from lattice quantum Gibbs states has found rigorous, efficient protocols. Nevertheless, exact Gibbs states, as the input premise, are in fact computationally intractable to prepare and may not faithfully represent generic finite-temperature quantum systems. In contrast, a system coupled to a heat bath can be stuck at an approximate stationary state (metastable state) long before it truly equilibrates. Here, we formulate a physically and algorithmically consistent alternative: learning from such metastable states of detailed-balanced master equations (Lindbladians) arising from system-bath interactions. We distill the algorithmic mechanism and structural condition underlying Gibbs-state learning and extend it in full to metastable states, attaining nearly optimal sample and computational complexity (in the system size and the precision). More broadly, we sharpen notions of metastability and develop a unified framework for finite-temperature learning.

</details>

Abstract: Nuclear quantum effects are critical for describing proton transfer and hydrogen bonding, but their incorporation into quantum chemistry calculations is often computationally prohibitive on classical hardware. A promising alternative are quantum computers due to their linear scaling in the space requirements with system size. We introduce a novel hybrid quantum-classical algorithm for ab-initio molecular dynamics that incorporates nuclear quantum effects via the nuclear-electronic orbital method. The proposed algorithm evaluates ground state energies and forces on the quantum computer using a variational quantum eigensolver, while the molecular geometries are updated classically. We validate our approach through simulations of $\text{H}_2$, $\text{H}_2\text{O}$ and the Zundel ion $\text{H}_5\text{O}_2^+$, comparing the simulated vibrational spectra with experimental data. Upon inclusion of nuclear quantum effects, the proton shuttling movement in the Zundel ion becomes effectively barrierless, and errors in the simulated frequencies improve significantly. Employing compact hardware-efficient ansatz circuits we achieve results comparable to the more accurate UCCSD ansatzes, which hints towards the feasibility of executing our algorithm on near-term quantum devices.

</details>

*Zhenhuan Liu,Zhenyu Du,Yifan Tang,Zi-Wen Liu,Jens Eisert,Ingo Roth*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种类似贝尔不等式的测试方法，利用前向访问与可信量子操作检测时间反演对称性：纠缠将时间输入-输出关系转化为可测量的空间交换对称性，并证明区分时间反演对称圆系综与Haar随机动力学所需的查询复杂度由探针与测量的纠缠负度决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间反演对称性是物理学基本原理，但如何用仅前向访问的量子操作对其进行有效检验尚缺定量框架。作者希望建立纠缠与时间反演对称性之间的联系，桥接量子信息科学与基础物理中的两个核心概念。

Method: 将时间反演对称性测试形式化为类贝尔不等式检验，仅使用前向访问和可信量子操作；对n量子比特酉动力学，分析区分时间反演对称圆系综与Haar随机动力学的问题；引入探针与测量的对数纠缠负度e_s、e_m，推导经典自适应协议下的查询复杂度下界，并对最大纠缠探针与SWAP测量进行优化构造。

Result: 证明任何经典自适应协议需Ω(min{2^{n/2},2^{n-e}})次查询，其中e=min{e_s,e_m}；最大纠缠探针与SWAP测量可将成本降至常数次查询；对任意固定兼容探针与测量给出测试方案，将其查询复杂度与对数纠缠负度关联，并在高纠缠区间通过优化探针与测量匹配下界标度。

Conclusion: 纠缠与时间反演对称性之间存在定量联系，所提测试框架为仅用前向访问检验反酉对称性提供了复杂度刻画与最优策略，连接了量子信息科学与基础物理。

Abstract: Time-reversal symmetry is a fundamental principle of physics describing the invariance of physical laws under reversal of the direction of time. We formulate a Bell-inequality-like test of this antiunitary symmetry using only forward access and trusted quantum operations: entanglement converts temporal input--output relations into measurable spatial exchange symmetry. For $n$-qubit unitary dynamics, we prove that reliably distinguishing the time-reversal-symmetric circular ensembles from Haar-random dynamics requires $Ω(\min\{2^{n/2},2^{n-e}\})$ queries for any classically adaptive protocol. Here, $e=\min\{e_{\mathrm s},e_{\mathrm m}\}$ with $e_{\mathrm s}$ and $e_{\mathrm m}$ representing the probe and measurement logarithmic entanglement negativities, respectively. Maximally entangled probes and SWAP measurements reduce this cost to a constant number of queries. Furthermore, we develop a time-reversal symmetry test for arbitrary fixed, compatible probes and measurements, relate its query complexity to their logarithmic negativities, and match the lower-bound scaling in the high-entanglement regime by optimizing the probe and measurement. Our results establish a quantitative connection between entanglement and time-reversal symmetry, bridging two central concepts in quantum information science and fundamental physics.

</details>


### [98] [Adversarial Robustness in Fake Quantum Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01574)
*Marc Maußner,Volker Reers*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究基于噪声模型仿真器的量子机器学习（QML）模型的性能可扩展性与对抗鲁棒性，分两阶段：先基准测试Qiskit Aer仿真引擎在不同硬件架构上的计算吞吐，再评估投影梯度下降（PGD）攻击与对抗重训练策略在真实噪声条件下的有效性。结果表明中等规模仿真（最多8量子比特）的运行时权衡与扩展行为，并证明高对抗/良性样本重训练比例（50/50）对4量子比特分类器在真实噪声下实现实用鲁棒性至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QML模型在真实量子硬件上受噪声影响，其性能扩展性与对抗鲁棒性尚不清晰；需在噪声模型仿真环境下量化仿真吞吐与对抗攻击/防御效果。

Method: 采用双阶段研究：第一阶段在多种硬件架构上对Qiskit Aer仿真引擎的计算吞吐进行基准测试；第二阶段在真实噪声条件下评估PGD攻击及对抗重训练策略；实验规模为中等规模仿真（最多8量子比特），并对比不同对抗/良性重训练比例。

Result: 量化了中等规模仿真（最多8量子比特）的运行时权衡与扩展行为；发现对4量子比特分类器而言，在真实噪声条件下，高对抗/良性重训练比例（50/50）是实现实用模型鲁棒性的必要条件。

Conclusion: Qiskit Aer在中等规模QML仿真中呈现明确的运行时与扩展权衡；在噪声条件下，提高对抗样本在重训练中的占比（如50/50）对4量子比特分类器的鲁棒性具有关键作用。

Abstract: This paper investigates the performance scalability and adversarial robustness of Quantum Machine Learning (QML) models deployed on noise-model-based fake simulators. We conduct a dual-phased study, first benchmarking the computational throughput of Qiskit's Aer simulation engine across varying hardware architectures, and second, evaluating the effectiveness of Projected Gradient Descent (PGD) attacks and adversarial retraining strategies under realistic noise conditions. Our results quantify the runtime tradeoffs and scaling behavior for medium-scale simulations (projected up to 8 qubits) and demonstrate that high adversarial-to-benign retraining ratios (50/50) are essential for achieving practical model robustness for 4-qubit classifiers under realistic noise conditions.

</details>


### [99] [Electromagnetic Side-Channel Vulnerability in QKD Equipment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01609)
*Mikio Fujiwara,Katsumi Fujii,Fumie Ono,Masakazu Ono,Akihisa Tomita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子密钥分发(QKD)网络节点的电磁侧信道泄露问题，在微波暗室内测量了基于time-bin诱骗态BB84协议原型设备的电磁辐射。结果表明发射端是主要泄露源，在1241.6 MHz时钟的三次谐波处可区分不同量子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际QKD网络节点可能面临电磁泄露等侧信道攻击威胁，需评估真实设备的电磁辐射特性以判断其安全性风险。

Method: 搭建含Y、Z基的time-bin诱骗态BB84协议QKD原型系统，在微波暗室内于距发射端和接收端0 m与3 m处测量电磁频谱及波形。

Result: 发射端为主要电磁泄露来源；在1241.6 MHz时钟的三次谐波频率上，不同量子态产生的电磁辐射存在明显差异。

Conclusion: QKD设备存在可被利用的电磁侧信道泄露，尤其发射端及特定谐波频段的量子态差异可能危及密钥安全性，需加强电磁屏蔽与防护。

Abstract: Actual quantum key distribution network nodes may be vulnerable to side-channel attacks such as electromagnetic leakage. In this study, we measured electromagnetic emissions from a prototype quantum key distribution device implementing a time-bin decoy-state BB84 protocol with Y and Z bases. We measured the electromagnetic spectra and waveforms at distances of 0 m and 3 m from the transmitter and receiver inside an anechoic chamber. The results showed that the transmitter was the primary source of electromagnetic leakage, and distinct differences were observed between the quantum states generated at the third harmonic of the 1241.6 MHz clock.

</details>


### [100] [Dissipation-Enabled Operator-Norm Locality Bounds for Bose-Hubbard Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01669)
*Marius Lemm,Tim Möbus*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明在具有在位ℓ-光子损耗（ℓ>2）的耗散Bose–Hubbard模型中，局部耗散可恢复算符范数的Lieb–Robinson界，并给出近乎弹道的传播界，进而得到局部通道近似、热力学极限与高效数字量子模拟等"对输入态一致"的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环（保守）玻色格点系统一般不满足对初始态一致的算符范数Lieb–Robinson界，因为信息传播速度会随局部玻色占据数增长，甚至宏观巨大。作者希望像量子自旋系统那样，获得对输入态一致的有效性保证，从而支持局部通道近似、热力学极限和数字量子模拟等应用。

Method: 研究由Lindblad算子描述的耗散Bose–Hubbard模型，含在位ℓ-光子损耗（ℓ>2）。利用耗散快速耗尽高占据格点，在Sobolev型局部粒子矩尺度上对状态进行正则化，建立局部矩界；这些界限在时间趋零时发散，但在足够低阶时在零点附近可积。进一步将该方法推广到cat-code耗散。

Result: 主要结果为近乎弹道的Lieb–Robinson界；在cat-code情形下，对处于码空间的初始态证明了总体积一致的局部绝热近似。作为推论，得到局部通道近似、热力学极限以及高效数字量子模拟的有效性。

Conclusion: 关键结论是：这些应用现在可以像量子自旋系统一样"对输入态一致"地成立，这与闭环Bose–Hubbard系统形成鲜明对比；局部耗散是恢复算符范数Lieb–Robinson界的关键机制。

Abstract: Closed bosonic lattice systems do not generally admit operator norm Lieb--Robinson bounds uniformly in the initial state. The reason is that the information propagation velocity can grow with the local boson occupancy, which can be macroscopically large. Here, we show that local dissipation restores an operator-norm Lieb--Robinson bound. We consider the dissipative Bose--Hubbard model described by a Lindbladian operator with on-site $\ell$-photon loss, $\ell>2$ and, for our main result, we establish an almost-ballistic Lieb-Robinson bound. Dissipation rapidly depletes highly occupied sites, thus regularizing the state on the Sobolev-type scale of local particle moments. The resulting moment bounds diverge as time approaches zero but remain integrable near zero at sufficiently low orders. We extend these ideas to treat cat-code dissipation and, for initial states in the code space, we prove a local adiabatic approximation uniform in the total volume. Further consequences include local channel approximation, a thermodynamic limit, and efficient digital quantum simulation. The point is that these applications are now available \textit{uniformly in the input state}, as for quantum spin systems, but in contrast to closed Bose--Hubbard systems.

</details>


### [101] [A Code-Agnostic Graph Neural Network Decoder from the Detection Error Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01683)
*Federico Alberto Astolfi,Guido Pupillo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 POLYMECHANON，一种仅以量子码的检测错误模型（DEM）为输入的图神经网络解码器，可跨稳定子码和噪声模型通用解码，在多种码上优于或匹配现有解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子纠错解码器通常针对特定码或噪声模型进行专门设计，缺乏通用性；且部分解码器（如 BP+OSD）的解码时间随物理错误率增长，难以满足实时解码需求。因此需要一种以码本身为数据、无需重新设计的通用解码架构。

Method: 将检测错误模型（DEM）表示为检测器、错误机制与逻辑可观测量的三部图，所有输入特征均由量子码计算得到而非人工设计；采用图神经网络（GNN）作为解码器，仅以 DEM 为输入，训练后可解码任意可表达为 DEM 的稳定子码及噪声模型。

Result: 在旋转表面码上，兼顾现象学与电路级噪声下均优于关联 MWPM，逻辑失败最多减少 25%；在高码率 qLDPC 码族上，小码匹配 BP+OSD，大码超越之，[\![130,4,6]\!] 上逻辑失败减少达 16%；解码时间不随物理错误率变化（单 GPU 约几毫秒/次，BP+OSD 单 CPU 核约几十毫秒）；单一模型跨码族训练后在图类码上优于非关联 MWPM，在其他码上落后 BP+OSD 不超过 10%，但对未见过的较大码泛化较差；概率输出支持基于置信度的后选择，表面码上逻辑错误率降低超过一个数量级且保留 90% 以上样本。

Conclusion: 仅依赖 DEM 变化即可解码新码与新噪声模型而无需重新设计解码器；其固定计算图使其成为中性原子处理器实时解码的候选方案。主要局限是对训练中未见过（尤其更大）的码泛化能力不足。

Abstract: We present POLYMECHANON, a graph neural network (GNN) decoder for quantum error correction whose only input is the detection error model (DEM) of a quantum code under a given noise model. We represent the DEM as a tripartite graph of detectors, error mechanisms and logical observables, where every input feature is computed by using the quantum code as data rather than design choice. In this way, the same architecture decodes in principle any stabiliser code, under any noise model that can be expressed as a detection error model, once trained on the DEM. We test this approach along four directions. Firstly, on the rotated surface code the decoder outperforms correlated MWPM under both phenomenological and circuit-level noise, with up to $25\%$ fewer logical failures. Secondly, on a family of high-rate qLDPC codes it matches BP+OSD on the smaller codes and surpasses it on the larger ones, with up to $16\%$ fewer logical failures on $[\![130,4,6]\!]$. Thirdly, its decoding time does not depend on the physical error rate, whereas that of BP+OSD grows with it: on $[\![130,4,6]\!]$ its effective cost per shot is of the order of a few ms on a single GPU against a few tens of milliseconds for BP+OSD on a single CPU core, and its fixed computational graph makes it a candidate for real-time decoding on neutral-atom processors. Finally, a single model trained across codes of different families outperforms uncorrelated MWPM on the graph-like codes seen during training and stays within $10\%$ of BP+OSD on the others, while it tends to fail to generalize to unseen codes, particularly larger ones. Its probabilistic output enables confidence-based post-selection, lowering the logical error rate by more than an order of magnitude on the surface code while keeping more than $90\%$ of the shots. Since only the DEM changes, new codes and noise models can be decoded without redesigning the decoder.

</details>


### [102] [Mentor-Initiated Asymmetric Bidirectional Quantum Teleportation Protocol for Arbitrary Qubit States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01684)
*Sugata Adhya,Benchao Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种由导师发起的双向非对称量子隐形传态协议，突破了现有方案对Bob→Alice两量子比特态须为Bell型态的限制，可实现任意两量子比特态的传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导师发起的双向非对称量子隐形传态方案中，Bob传给Alice的两量子比特态被限制为类Bell形式α|00⟩+β|11⟩，通用性不足，无法传输任意两量子比特态。

Method: 构建导师发起的双向非对称量子隐形传态协议框架；使用标准量子门构造所需纠缠资源；并在Qiskit中给出量子线路实现以演示协议流程。

Result: 协议允许在导师发起框架下将任意两量子比特态从Bob隐形传态至Alice，且纠缠资源可由标准量子门构造，并已通过Qiskit量子线路实现进行验证展示。

Conclusion: 该协议推广了导师发起型双向非对称量子隐形传态的适用范围，摆脱了类Bell态输入限制，具备可电路实现性。

Abstract: In this paper, a mentor-initiated bidirectional asymmetric quantum teleportation protocol is proposed. Unlike existing schemes that restrict the two-qubit state transmitted from Bob to Alice to a Bell-like form $α|00\rangle+β|11\rangle$, our protocol allows an arbitrary two-qubit state to be teleported from Bob to Alice within the mentor-initiated framework. The required entangled resources are constructed using standard quantum gates, and the protocol is illustrated through its quantum-circuit implementation in Qiskit.

</details>


### [103] [Predicting electric-field noise in ion traps using fluctuation electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01693)
*Markus Teller,Da An,Alberto M. Alonso,Philip C. Holz,Philipp Schindler,Hartmut Häffner,Tracy E. Northup*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预测微加工表面离子阱中由介质与金属材料体涨落引起的电场噪声的方法，仅需材料能量损耗这一属性，可适用于任意阱几何。应用于含电气浮置电极的离子阱时，介质贡献占主导，模型预测噪声与实测值相差不超过4倍，并进一步分析了典型表面阱设计中电极结构的噪声屏蔽作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微加工表面离子阱中的电场噪声是俘获离子量子计算机门错误的重要来源，但该噪声长期以来难以预测，缺乏通用且基于材料本征属性的理论工具。

Method: 构建一种从介质与金属材料体涨落出发预测电场噪声的方法，适用于任意阱几何，且唯一相关材料属性为能量损耗；将该方法应用于带有电气浮置电极的离子阱，并推广至典型表面阱设计以分析电极结构的噪声屏蔽效应。

Result: 在含浮置电极的离子阱中，介质对噪声的贡献占主导；模型预测的噪声与该阱实测噪声在4倍以内吻合；对典型表面阱设计的分析揭示了电极结构对噪声的屏蔽规律。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种几何通用、仅依赖材料能量损耗的电场噪声预测框架，能较好复现实验观测，并可用于指导表面离子阱电极结构的低噪声设计。

Abstract: Electric-field noise in microfabricated surface ion traps contributes to gate errors in trapped-ion quantum computers, but this noise has been challenging to predict. Here, we present a method to predict the electric-field noise arising from fluctuations in the bulk of dielectric and metallic materials. The method is valid for arbitrary trap geometries, and the only relevant material property is energy loss. We apply it to an ion trap with an electrically floating electrode and find the contribution from dielectrics to be dominant. Our model predicts the observed noise for this trap within a factor of four. In addition, we apply our model to typical surface trap designs and analyze how the electrode structures shield the noise.

</details>


### [104] [Efficiently Optimizing the Quantum Value of Bell Inequalities using Batched Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01699)
*Xinyu Xu,Ping Zhu,Weikang Li,Pierre Pocreau,Dalu Ding,Dawei Ding*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于批量梯度下降（BGD）的贝尔不等式量子值优化器，通过可微搜索可行量子策略并利用GPU并行随机重启，显著加速大规模贝尔不等式的数值求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用优化器（如see-saw方法）难以处理仅有十几个输入输出的贝尔不等式，无法满足研究大规模贝尔不等式、设计实验及随机性生成、多智能体协调等应用需求，因此需要能随方数、输入、输出有效扩展的数值方法。

Method: 提出BGD优化器：在可行量子策略空间上进行可微搜索，态和投影测量由无约束变量生成；贝尔表达式通过直接张量收缩求值，避免构建过大的贝尔算子；该形式使并行随机重启成本低廉，天然适配GPU。同时用GPU枚举策略或将优化表达为混合整数线性规划来高效计算经典值。

Result: 与see-saw方法相比，在多种贝尔不等式族上取得显著加速；在GPU上可在数分钟内优化含上千输入或输出的贝尔不等式，并能高效计算这些大规模不等式的经典值。

Conclusion: 该优化器可作为贝尔实验及多智能体协调问题的数值评估工具，适用于高频交易、分布式系统等具有大量输入输出特征的现实场景。

Abstract: Exploring the world of Bell inequalities requires numerical methods that scale effectively with the number of parties, inputs, and outputs. Such methods can be used to study large-scale Bell inequalities beyond the reach of analytic methods, to design experiments for violating more complicated Bell inequalities, and to enable applications of Bell inequality violation. Such applications include randomness generation and multi-agent coordination with restricted communication. However, current general purpose optimizers such as the see-saw method struggle to handle Bell inequalities with only a dozen inputs and outputs. We introduce an optimizer for the quantum value of a Bell inequality based on batched gradient descent (BGD). More precisely, our BGD optimizer is a differentiable search over feasible quantum strategies in which states and projective measurements are generated from unconstrained variables. The Bell expression is evaluated by a direct tensor contraction without forming the prohibitively large Bell operator. This formulation makes parallel random restarts inexpensive and is thus naturally implemented by a GPU. We evaluate our optimizer against the see-saw method and find a significant speedup for a wide variety of Bell inequality families. Implemented on a GPU, our BGD optimizer can optimize Bell inequalities in our numerical experiments with more than a thousand inputs or outputs within minutes. For these large-scale Bell inequalities, we also efficiently compute the classical value by enumerating possible strategies using a GPU or expressing the optimization as a mixed-integer linear program. Our optimizers can serve as numerical evaluation tools for Bell experiments and multi-agent coordination problems modeled by Bell inequalities with many inputs and outputs, a common feature of real-world scenarios such as high frequency trading and distributed systems.

</details>


### [105] [Less precise but less noisy: local circuits for momentum-space state preparation and measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01704)
*Etienne Granet,Henrik Dreyer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在H2量子计算机上比较紧密束缚链基态制备，发现存在临界系统尺寸N，超过后绝热演化以相同门数和电路深度达到比FFT更低的能量，原因是FFT的高动量分辨率需长程耦合，误差传播更快、噪声敏感度更高。作者提出一种更低成本、更低噪声的动量测量方案，在谱函数测量中优于FFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法优化通常以门数或电路深度为指标，但忽视了噪声敏感度这一关键因素。作者旨在揭示超越门数与深度之外的噪声敏感度对算法性能的重要影响，并寻找在真实量子硬件上性能更优的方案。

Method: 在Quantinuum System Model H2量子计算机上进行紧密束缚链基态制备实验，在相同门数和相同电路深度下对比绝热演化与Fermionic Fourier Transform (FFT)的能量表现；并通过分析FFT高动量分辨率（可区分1/N的动量）与实空间长程耦合之间的关系来解释噪声敏感度差异；进而设计一种精度较低但成本更低、噪声更小的动量测量方案用于谱函数测量。

Result: 在H2量子计算机上，存在系统尺寸N，超过该尺寸后绝热演化以相同门数和电路深度达到显著低于FFT的能量。FFT因高精度能区分1/N的动量而噪声敏感度高，其动量空间高分辨率对应实空间长程耦合，使误差传播更快；而绝热演化等局域物理电路动量分辨率较粗但误差传播更慢。新提出的动量测量方案虽精度低于FFT，但成本更低、噪声更小，在H2上的谱函数测量中性能优于FFT。

Conclusion: 对于物理应用，高动量分辨率很少被需要，通常值得用其换取低噪声敏感度。研究强调在门数和电路深度之外，降低量子算法噪声敏感度的重要性。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms are usually optimized for gate count or circuit depth. We find on Quantinuum System Model H2 quantum computer that for a tight-binding chain ground state preparation, there is a system size $N$ beyond which the adiabatic evolution reaches significantly lower energies than the Fermionic Fourier Transform (FFT), with the same number of gates, and with the same circuit depth. We attribute this high noise sensitivity of the FFT to its high precision, being able to distinguish momenta by $1/N$. This high resolution in momentum space requires long-range couplings in real space, which propagates errors faster. In contrast, although local and physical circuits such as the adiabatic evolution have a coarser momentum resolution, they also propagate errors more slowly. For physical applications, high momentum resolution is rarely required and is often worth trading for low noise sensitivity. We also introduce a momentum measurement scheme that although less precise than FFT, is less costly and less noisy. We show that it achieves better performance than FFT for spectral function measurement on Quantinuum System Model H2 quantum computer. Our work emphasizes the importance of reducing the noise sensitivity of quantum algorithms, beyond the number of gates or circuit depth.

</details>


### [106] [Near-optimal quantum query lower bounds on bipartiteness and expansion testing in the bounded-degree graph model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01752)
*Chandrima Kayal,Sayantan Sen,Dániel Szabó*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有界度模型下研究二部性检测与扩张性检测的量子查询复杂度，证明了两者均有 $\widetilde{\Omega}(N^{1/3})$ 的量子查询下界，从而完全刻画了这两个问题近乎最优的量子查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二部性与扩张性检测是图性质检测中的经典问题。经典情形下两者均已知为 $\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{N})$；量子情形下 Ambainis, Childs 和 Liu (2011) 给出了 $\widetilde{O}(N^{1/3})$ 的算法并证明扩张性检测需要 $\widetilde{\Omega}(N^{1/4})$ 下界，但二部性检测是否具有指数级量子优势一直悬而未决，且此后十余年无进展。本文旨在填补这一下界缺口。

Method: 采用多项式方法（polynomial method），与 Ambainis, Childs 和 Liu 类似，但引入中间问题（intermediate problems）并通过归约将其与主问题关联，同时对所得多项式进行更精细的分析，以获得近乎最优的下界。

Result: 证明了二部性检测与扩张性检测的 $\widetilde{\Omega}(N^{1/3})$ 量子查询下界，与已有上界相匹配，从而在多项式对数因子范围内完全刻画了这两个问题的量子查询复杂度。

Conclusion: 二部性和扩张性检测问题在有界度模型下的量子查询复杂度均为 $\widetilde{\Theta}(N^{1/3})$；该结果否定了二部性检测存在指数级量子优势的可能性，并解决了长期悬而未决的下界问题。

Abstract: In this work, we study bipartiteness and expansion testing, two canonical problems in graph property testing in the bounded-degree model through the lens of quantum query complexity. In the classical setting, it is known that $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{N})$ queries are necessary and sufficient for both these testing problems (Goldreich and Ron, 1999, 2000 & 2002), where $N$ denotes the number of vertices of the input graph. Due to their significance, (Ambainis, Childs, and Liu, 2011) initiated the study of these problems in the quantum setting and designed quantum algorithms for bipartiteness and expansion testing that perform $\widetilde{O}(N^{1/3})$ queries, showing a polynomial speedup. They also proved that $\widetildeΩ(N^{1/4})$ queries are necessary for expansion testing, but the possibility of an exponential quantum advantage for bipartiteness testing remained open. Despite significant effort, there has been no improvement in these results in the last decade and a half. In this work, we prove essentially tight $\widetildeΩ(N^{1/3})$ quantum query lower bounds for both bipartiteness and expansion testing, thereby completely characterizing the quantum query complexity of these problems up to polylogarithmic factors. While our proofs use the polynomial method similarly to Ambainis, Childs, and Liu, we use intermediate problems that we relate to the main problems via reductions, and perform a more precise analysis of the resulting polynomials, leading to the near-optimal lower bounds.

</details>


### [107] [Efficient quantum phase estimation with adaptive entanglement-assisted Hadamard test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01772)
*Hengzhun Chen,Benchi Zhao,Yingzhou Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出自适应纠缠辅助Hadamard测试（AEHT），通过迭代精炼参考相位实现渐进式放大，抑制不完美本征态制备引起的系统偏差，并以器件重启次数为成本度量，在近量子处理器上高效估计高精度量子相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠辅助Hadamard测试（EHT）的相位放大受参考相位精度根本性限制，在高精度区间效率低下；同时在不完美本征态制备场景下常规EHT存在不可避免的系统偏差；此外现有成本度量（shot数）未能反映实际物理实现代价。

Method: 提出自适应纠缠辅助Hadamard测试（AEHT）：迭代地精炼参考相位，使放大强度随迭代逐步增强；针对不完美本征态制备场景抑制系统偏差；采用器件重启次数（device-restart count）替代shot数作为量子相位估计的成本度量，并进行数值实验与常规方法对比。

Result: 数值实验证实，在以器件重启次数为成本度量下，所提AEHT相比常规方法有效；AEHT能够抑制不完美本征态制备带来的系统偏差，并释放大纠缠态的完整放大能力。

Conclusion: AEHT通过迭代精炼参考相位释放大纠缠态放大能力，为近量子处理器上高精度量子相位估计提供了高效方法。

Abstract: The entanglement-assisted Hadamard test (EHT) is a practical method for estimating a quantum phase by amplifying the phase signal. However, the feasible amplification is fundamentally limited by the accuracy of the reference phase, such that the method is inefficient in the high-precision regime. In this work, we propose an algorithm, called adaptive entanglement-assisted Hadamard test (AEHT), that iteratively refines the reference phase, enabling progressively stronger amplification as the iteration goes by. We further consider the imperfect eigenstate preparation scenario, where a systematic bias is unavoidable when estimating the quantum phase with the conventional EHT. Such a bias can be suppressed by the proposed AEHT. Moreover, taking physical implementation into consideration, we adopt device-restart count to measure the cost of quantum phase estimation, rather than shot count. The numerical experiments confirm the effectiveness of the proposed AEHT compared with conventional methods under this measure. By unlocking the full amplification power of large entangled states, this work offers an efficient method to estimate high-precision quantum phase on near-term quantum processors.

</details>


### [108] [QUFIG: GNN-Based Prediction of Quantum Fault Injection Vulnerabilities with Gate-Level Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01777)
*Shihan Zhao,Qiying Li,Ben Dong,Qian Wang,Yuntao Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出QUFIG框架，将门级故障分析形式化为受限保真度预算下的学习引导优先级排序问题，用基于电路DAG的GNN预测每个门对不同故障类型的脆弱性分数并排序，从而高效定位高影响脆弱门-故障对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件规模扩大与可访问性提高带来新的可靠性与安全挑战，例如云量子计算平台中的运行时故障注入攻击。现有工作无法以门级精度识别脆弱性，也难以适应运行时环境。

Method: 将门级故障分析建模为受限保真度预算下的学习引导优先级排序问题；采用基于电路DAG的GNN骨干网络，预测每个门对每类注入故障的脆弱性分数（定义为门-故障对对电路保真度的影响），并据此对门-故障对排序。

Result: 在QASMbench和HamLib MaxCut上，QUFIG以少于随机和基于深度启发式的检查次数恢复高影响脆弱门-故障对；相比基线可将需检查的门数量减少2.9–19.8%，同时保持有效的故障识别。

Conclusion: QUFIG能够帮助量子电路设计者更高效地识别脆弱性并施加有针对性的防御。

Abstract: The growing scale and accessibility of quantum hardware exposed new reliability and security challenges in the quantum computing workflow, such as the run-time fault injection attacks in cloud-based quantum computing platforms. However, existing works fail to identify vulnerabilities with gate-level precision or adapt to run-time environments. In this work, we formulate gate-level fault analysis as a learning-guided prioritization problem under restricted fidelity budgets. The framework uses a circuit-DAG-based GNN backbone to predict the vulnerability score of each gate to each type of injected fault, defined as the impact of the gate-fault pair on circuit fidelity. The gate-fault pairs are then ranked by their vulnerability score. Experiments on QASMbench and HamLib MaxCut show that QUFIG recovers high-impact vulnerable gate-fault pairs with fewer inspections than random and depth-based heuristics. Our results show that QUFIG can reduce the number of gates requiring inspection by 2.9--19.8% while maintaining effective fault identification, allowing quantum circuit designers to identify vulnerabilities and apply targeted defenses more efficiently.

</details>


### [109] [Non-Markovian effects on informational steady states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01782)
*Jacob Werner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非马尔可夫效应对信息稳态（ISSs）的影响，采用连续监测碰撞模型，发现稳态信息增益与非马尔可夫度呈负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息稳态（ISSs）已在马尔可夫情形下有研究，但记忆效应如何影响其形成与稳态性质尚不清楚，因此需要探究非马尔可夫效应。

Method: 在连续监测碰撞模型框架下，将环境建模为一系列双量子比特辅助粒子（ancillas）。

Result: 对该特定模型，每次测量的稳态信息增益与非马尔可夫度呈负相关；数值结果进一步刻画了系统-辅助粒子以及辅助粒子-辅助粒子相互作用参数如何影响非马尔可夫度与稳态信息增益。

Conclusion: 记忆效应（非马尔可夫性）会降低稳态信息增益，且该效应可通过模型中的相互作用参数进行调控。

Abstract: Informational steady states (ISSs) arise when the rate at which information is gained from continuous measurements is exactly balanced by the rate at which information from earlier measurements is lost to the environment. While ISSs have been studied in Markovian settings, relatively little is known about how memory effects influence their formation and steady-state properties. In this work, we investigate non-Markovian effects on ISSs within the framework of continuously monitored collision models. We model the environment as a sequence of two-qubit ancillas and find that, for this specific model, the steady-state information gain per measurement is negatively correlated with the degree of non-Markovianity. Numerical results further characterize how the system-ancilla and ancilla-ancilla interaction parameters influence both the degree of non-Markovianity and the steady-state information gain.

</details>


### [110] [Learnt Attacks on Quantum Key Distribution under Channel Noise and Device Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01792)
*Marcel Mordarski,Benjamin Gras,Abdelrahman Shehata,Daniel Budina,Roberto Bondesan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将自适应窃听形式化为受约束的马尔可夫决策过程，攻击者在Ornstein–Uhlenbeck漂移信道下逐轮选择门电路，并给出自适应收益的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QKD链路的安全性基于静态信道分析，但设备在两次重校准之间的信道漂移问题尚未被量化：无法改变信道自身噪声的窃听者能否利用该漂移获益仍不清楚。同时现有学习型攻击依赖固定门模板与固定信道，难以推广到无已知模板的噪声模型。

Method: 将自适应窃听建模为受约束的马尔可夫决策过程（每块轮次的预算作为中止条件），噪声服从Ornstein–Uhlenbeck过程；攻击者每轮选择一个电路。用强化学习联合搜索门结构与旋转角度，使电路足够紧凑以构成离散动作集，并推广到幅度阻尼信道等无模板噪声模型。以最佳固定电路与动态规划上界夹逼自适应收益。

Result: 在双边退极化噪声的器件无关E91上，RL攻击者将Holevo信息从最佳固定电路的0.135提升至零探测下的0.348，达上界的98%；在漂移比特翻转信道BB84上，其保真度超过保守的噪声索引规则0.024，达上界的99%。静态噪声下，基不对称带来的收益在平均误差率与逐基误差率约束之间变号。从随机门序列出发的搜索可复原解析克隆机与集体攻击密钥率，并从上方满足Winick–Lütkenhaus–Coles目标下界。

Conclusion: 自适应窃听的价值被严格界定在最优固定电路与动态规划上界之间，学习型攻击可逼近该上界并统一解释已知最优攻击，说明信道漂移对QKD实际安全性构成可量化的威胁。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) links are provisioned from security analyses of stationary channels, whereas the devices that determine the channel drift between recalibrations. Whether an eavesdropper who cannot alter the channel's own noise gains by following that drift has not been quantified. Adaptive eavesdropping is posed here as a constrained Markov decision process in which the attacker selects one circuit per round while the noise level follows an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process and the abort condition is a budget over each block of rounds. The value of adaptation is bounded by the best fixed circuit and a dynamic-programming upper bound. The actions are learnt attacks. Whereas Decker et al. trained a parametrised circuit on a fixed gate template against a fixed channel, here the gate structure and rotation angles are searched jointly. This yields circuits compact enough to form a discrete action set, extending the construction to noise models lacking a known template, including the amplitude damping channel. On device-independent E91 under bilateral depolarising noise, a reinforcement-learning attacker raises her Holevo information from $0.135$ for the best fixed circuit to $0.348$ at zero detection, $98\%$ of the upper bound. On BB84 under a drifting bit-flip channel, she exceeds a conservative noise-indexed rule by $0.024$ in fidelity, reaching $99\%$ of the upper bound. Under stationary noise, the attacker's gain from basis asymmetry changes sign between an averaged and a per-basis error-rate constraint. The search, started from random gate sequences, recovers the analytical cloners and the collective-attack key rate, and meets the lower bound of the Winick--Lütkenhaus--Coles objective from above.

</details>


### [111] [Collision models: Markovian and Non-Markovian impurity models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01814)
*Jacob Werner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一类"马尔可夫杂质模型"(碰撞模型),用BLP度量证明非马尔可夫性随杂质数p单调,并数值研究临界p与相互作用强度的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞模型是研究开放量子系统与引入非马尔可夫性的灵活框架;物理上存在信息回流被周期性阻断的情形,需要相应的模型刻画。

Method: 构建"马尔可夫杂质模型"这一新型碰撞模型;采用Breuer-Laine-Piilo(BLP)非马尔可夫性度量进行分析;通过数值模拟研究临界参数p对系统-辅助系统(system-ancilla)与辅助系统间(ancilla-ancilla)相互作用强度的依赖。

Result: 若p-杂质模型为非马尔可夫,则(p+1)-杂质模型亦为非马尔可夫,故存在由马尔可夫向非马尔可夫转变的临界p。数值表明:虽存在例外,但增强任一相互作用强度总体会降低临界p;当辅助系统间相互作用足够弱时,即使无杂质动力学仍保持马尔可夫性。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫杂质模型为研究周期性阻断信息回流的开放系统提供了统一框架,揭示了临界杂质数的存在及其对相互作用强度的依赖;并简要提出互补的非马尔可夫杂质模型。

Abstract: Collision models provide a flexible framework for studying open quantum systems and introducing non-Markovianity. We present a novel class of collision models, which we term Markovian impurity models, motivated by physical scenarios in which information backflow is periodically blocked. Using the Breuer-Laine-Piilo (BLP) measure of non-Markovianity, we show that if a $p$-impurity model is non-Markovian, then the corresponding $(p+1)$-impurity model is also non-Markovian, implying the existence of a critical $p$ at which the process transitions from Markovian to non-Markovian. Numerical simulations are used to investigate how this critical parameter depends on the system-ancilla and ancilla-ancilla interaction strengths. We find that, altough exceptions exist, increasing either interaction strength generally lowers the critical $p$, while sufficiently weak ancilla-ancilla interactions lead to a regime in which the dynamics remain Markovian even in the absence of impurities. Finally, we briefly introduce the complementary non-Markovian impurity model.

</details>


### [112] [QPI-DeepONet-MAC: A Scalable and Stable Hybrid Classical-Quantum Architecture for Physics-Informed Deep Operator Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01824)
*Said Lantigua,José Valencia,Gilson Giraldi,Renato Portugal,Jonas Maziero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出混合量子-经典架构QPI-DeepONet-MAC，通过乘加耦合将参数化量子电路融入PI-DeepONets，并给出通用逼近、无贫瘠高原、传感器下界及相干性正则化等理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化PDE的通用算子学习是AI与物理建模交叉的核心难题。现有PI-DeepONets在高维下优化困难，量子扩展虽增强表示能力却受贫瘠高原制约，缺乏可训练性与采样复杂度的理论保障。

Method: 提出QPI-DeepONet-MAC混合量子-经典架构，用乘加耦合（MAC）将经典表示与量子期望值结合；解析证明通用算子逼近能力；推导量子参数梯度界，给出避免贫瘠高原的条件与可训练性标度准则；推导达到目标精度所需传感器数量下界；提出基于量子相干与态保真度动力学的信息正则化方案。

Result: 证明架构保持通用算子逼近；建立量子参数梯度界，给出显式避免贫瘠高原条件及可训练性标度判据；得到传感器数量下界，将其与输入函数正则性、域维数和PDE动力学增长直接关联；信息正则化可防止训练中相干性丢失并保持量子结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子增强物理信息算子学习奠定理论基础，将QPI-DeepONet-MAC定位为求解参数化PDE的可扩展、稳定框架。

Abstract: General operator learning for parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) is a fundamental challenge at the intersection of artificial intelligence and physics-based modeling. Physics-informed Deep Operator Networks (PI-DeepONets) incorporate governing equations into learning, but face optimization difficulties in high dimensions. Quantum extensions offer additional representational capacity, yet their trainability can be hindered by barren plateaus. Here, we introduce QPI-DeepONet-MAC, a hybrid quantum-classical architecture that integrates parameterized quantum circuits into PI-DeepONets through a multiplicative-and-additive coupling (MAC) of classical representations and quantum expectation values. We prove analytically that the proposed architecture retains universal operator approximation capabilities. Furthermore, we derive bounds on quantum parameter gradients, establishing explicit conditions to avoid barren plateaus alongside a scaling criterion for trainability. Additionally, we derive a lower bound on the number of sensors required for a target accuracy, directly linking sampling requirements to input function regularity, domain dimension, and PDE dynamical growth. Finally, we propose an informational regularization scheme based on quantum coherence and state fidelity dynamics to prevent coherence loss and preserve quantum structure during training. Together, these results establish a theoretical foundation for quantum-enhanced physics-informed operator learning and position QPI-DeepONet-MAC as a scalable, stable framework for solving parametric PDEs.

</details>


### [113] [Experimental realization of Wheeler's delayed-choice experiment with dual selections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01832)
*Xiaowan Yang,Xinglei Yu,Liangsheng Li,Xinzhi Zhao,Tianle Zheng,Chengjie Zhang,Chuan-Feng Li,Guang-Can Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于纠缠光子实现双选择的延迟选择实验，通过路径编码同时控制Mach-Zehnder干涉仪中两个分束器的有/无，揭示了不同分束器配置下单光子的波粒行为，并讨论了分束器处于有/无量子叠加时光子呈现波粒量子叠加态的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wheeler延迟选择实验用于展示粒子的波粒二象性，但以往量子版本通常仅控制第二个分束器的有/无。本文希望将控制扩展到Mach-Zehnder干涉仪中的两个分束器，实现"双选择"，从而更全面地考察不同分束器配置对单光子波粒行为的影响，并进一步探讨分束器处于量子叠加时对应的波粒量子叠加态。

Method: 采用基于纠缠光子的延迟选择实验方案，利用路径编码同时控制Mach-Zehnder干涉仪中两个分束器的存在与缺失，实现双选择的实验配置，并观测单光子在不同双分束器配置下的波粒行为；进一步从理论上讨论两分束器处于有/无量子叠加时的情形。

Result: 实验揭示了单光子在不同双分束器配置下的波粒行为；并指出当两个分束器处于有与无的量子叠加时，光子将处于波与粒子的量子叠加态。

Conclusion: 通过纠缠光子与路径编码，本文在Mach-Zehnder干涉仪中实现了对两个分束器的延迟选择双控制，拓展了Wheeler延迟选择实验，并给出分束器量子叠加导致光子波粒量子叠加态的讨论。

Abstract: Wheeler's delayed-choice experiment demonstrates wave-particle duality of particles using different experimental configurations of a Mach-Zehnder interferometer. In a quantum version of this experiment, the wave-particle behavior of photons can be observed by controlling presence or absence of the second beam splitter. Here, we implement a delayed-choice experiment with dual selections based on entangled photons, experimentally controlling both of two beam splitters in the Mach-Zehnder interferometer, where the presence and absence of the second beam splitter are simultaneously controlled through path encoding. Our experiment reveals the wave-particle behavior of single photons under different configurations of two beam splitters. We further discuss the scenario when two beam splitters are in a quantum superposition of being present and absent, photons will be in a wave-particle quantum superposition state.

</details>


### [114] [Toward Optimal Circuit Depth for Geometrically Local Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01839)
*Zhenyu Shen,Yusen Wu,Penghui Yao,Xiao Yuan,Yukun Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究晶格上有限程哈密顿量动力学模拟的量子线路深度，构造出深度O(T log(nT/ε))、门数O(nT log(nT/ε))的最近邻线路，将先前polylog开销降至单个对数；并证明XXZ模型下深度下界Ω(log n/log log n)，达到近乎最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟的核心目标是理解相互作用量子系统的动力学。几何局域性暗示数字量子模拟应能保留底层动力学的并行性，但已有算法（如Haah-Hastings-Kothari-Low）存在较大的polylogarithmic线路深度开销，需要进一步压缩深度并厘清局域性与精度对线路复杂度的作用。

Method: 基于Lieb-Robinson分解将系统划分为对数尺度块；关键观察是空间局域性同时控制高精度成本。在每个块内，用常数阶乘积公式并加以修正项来补偿误差，从而获得指数级精度；修正项的实现深度与块直径成正比，不引入超出空间局域性所要求的额外渐近深度。同时针对均匀XXZ哈密顿量构造无条件下界证明。

Result: 构造出最近邻量子线路，模拟n量子比特、时间无关、有限程哈密顿量（固定维度晶格，归一化局域相互作用强度，时间T≥1）的动力学，在精度ε下线路深度为O(T log(nT/ε))，门数为O(nT log(nT/ε))，将polylog开销降为单个对数。并证明均匀XXZ哈密顿量在固定演化时间和足够小固定误差下，任何最近邻线路深度需Ω(log n/log log n)，与上界仅差双对数因子。

Conclusion: 研究厘清了局域性与精度在决定量子动力学线路复杂度中的作用，表明上述晶格哈密顿量模拟在该区间内线路深度近乎最优，并提示了空间受限量子计算的更广泛原则。

Abstract: Understanding the dynamics of interacting quantum systems is a central goal of quantum simulation. For geometrically local Hamiltonians, locality suggests that digital simulation should retain much of the parallelism of the underlying dynamics. For a target accuracy $ε$, we construct a nearest-neighbor quantum circuit that simulates the dynamics of an $n$-qubit, time-independent, finite-range Hamiltonian with normalized local interaction strength on a fixed-dimensional lattice for time $T\ge1$, with circuit depth $O(T\log(nT/ε))$ and gate count $O(nT\log(nT/ε))$. This reduces the polylogarithmic overhead of Haah, Hastings, Kothari, and Low to a single logarithm. On logarithmic-scale blocks from the Lieb--Robinson decomposition, a shallow but highly accurate implementation is essential for reducing the circuit depth. Our key observation is that spatial localization also controls the cost of high precision. In each block, the error of a constant-order product formula is compensated for by a correction, yielding exponential accuracy. The correction can be implemented with depth proportional to the block diameter, introducing no additional asymptotic depth scale beyond that required by spatial localization.
  Furthermore, we prove an unconditional circuit-depth lower bound for uniform XXZ Hamiltonians: at fixed evolution time $T$ in the stated regime and sufficiently small fixed error $ε$, any nearest-neighbor quantum circuit simulating these dynamics requires depth $Ω(\log n/\log\log n)$. This matches our $O(\log n)$ upper bound up to a doubly logarithmic factor, establishing near-optimal circuit depth for lattice Hamiltonian simulation in this regime. Together, these results clarify the role of locality and precision in determining the circuit complexity of quantum dynamics and suggest broader principles for spatially constrained quantum computation.

</details>


### [115] [Trapdoored Clifford Operators and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01848)
*Minki Hhan,Hojune Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"陷门Clifford算子分布"——其样本在计算上与均匀随机Clifford不可区分，但在已知陷门下可近线性时间采样与实现，并用于加速基于随机Clifford的协议及建立矩阵与Clifford问题的worst-case to average-case归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀随机n量子比特Clifford算子的采样与实现因Clifford群规模庞大而需近二次复杂度，成为随机基准测试、经典阴影、量子认证等应用的瓶颈；因此需要一种既能模拟均匀随机性又能高效实现的构造。

Method: 基于learning parity with noise（LPN）假设的一个变体，构造陷门Clifford算子分布：样本与均匀随机Clifford计算不可区分，已知陷门时可在近线性时间采样与实现；支持Pauli标签上的快速tableau作用以便经典模拟，并可优化至多对数深度实现；同时构造有限域上的陷门矩阵，支持与矩阵及其逆的高效乘法。

Result: 在LPN变体假设下实现近线性时间采样与实现的陷门Clifford分布，支持快速经典模拟与polylogarithmic深度实现；构造出支持与矩阵及其逆高效乘法的有限域陷门矩阵，解决了Vaikuntanathan和Zamir [SODA'26]遗留的开放问题；据此获得更快的随机Clifford协议。

Conclusion: 陷门Clifford分布可加速随机Clifford协议，并为矩阵与Clifford问题的worst-case to average-case归约提供工具；特别地，批处理Clifford电路（对多个寄存器施加同一Clifford）的合成至少与worst-case矩阵乘法一样难，即使合成仅在随机Clifford的小常数比例上成功，该结论亦可推广至一般量子电路的近似实现。

Abstract: Random Clifford operators have numerous applications in quantum computing, including randomized benchmarking, classical shadows, and quantum authentication. However, sampling and implementing uniformly random $n$-qubit Clifford incur near-quadratic complexity due to the size of Clifford group.
  We introduce a cryptographic way to overcome these barriers: trapdoored Clifford operator distributions whose samples are computationally indistinguishable from uniformly random Cliffords, yet implementing them can be much faster given the trapdoor. We construct a distribution of trapdoored Clifford operators whose elements can be sampled and implemented in near-linear time under a variant of the learning parity with noise assumption. Our constructions allow fast tableau action on Pauli labels for classical simulation, and also can be optimized to admit polylogarithmic-depth implementation. Along the way, we construct trapdoored matrices over finite fields that support efficient multiplication by both a matrix and its inverse, resolving an open question left by Vaikuntanathan and Zamir [SODA'26].
  We use these constructions to obtain faster protocols based on random Cliffords. We also explore their applications to the worst-case to average-case reductions for matrix and Clifford problems including the iterated matrix multiplication and Clifford circuit synthesis. In particular, we show the hardness of batching Clifford circuits: synthesizing circuits that apply the same Clifford to multiple registers is at least as hard as worst-case matrix multiplication, even when synthesis succeeds on a small constant fraction of random Cliffords. This extends to approximate implementations by general quantum circuits.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum Optimal Transport Barycenters: Existence, Duality, and Gaussian Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01855)
*Augusto Gerolin,Zhiyi Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建量子最优传输（QOT）重心框架，证明其存在性与对偶性，并在高斯输入下建立全局刚性定理：只要至少一个高斯输入是忠实的，重心在所有量子态中唯一且必为高斯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典 Wasserstein 重心的理论推广到量子态情形，统一处理 2-量子 Wasserstein（QW）重心在量子态与量子信道两种表述下的框架，并厘清高斯输入是否迫使 2-QW 重心本身为高斯且唯一这一核心问题。

Method: 在可分希尔伯特空间上，对一类可能无界的传输代价建立 QOT 重心的存在性与对偶性；通过选取 Caglioti–Golse–Mouhot–Paul 与 De Palma–Trevisan 的 2-QW 距离对应的典范二次代价算子，统一两种表述；将高斯输入下的重心问题化为协方差矩阵上的有限维凸优化，并借助协方差互补松弛下的态重构原理，把最优协方差的唯一性提升为整个量子态的唯一性。

Result: 重心问题存在高斯极小元，可归约为协方差矩阵的有限维凸优化；建立全局刚性定理：若至少一个高斯输入为忠实态，则重心在所有量子态中唯一且必为高斯。忠实性是充分但非必要条件：纯态族仍确定唯一重心，而部分纯的非忠实高斯输入可能出现多重重心。

Conclusion: QOT 重心框架为量子态与量子信道下的 2-QW 重心提供了统一处理，并通过态重构原理解决了"最优协方差唯一不蕴含量子态唯一"的关键困难，给出高斯刚性的一般判据。

Abstract: We develop a Quantum Optimal Transport (QOT) barycenter framework for quantum states, as an analog of Wasserstein barycenters~\cite{AguCar}, and establish existence and duality results for a broad class of possibly unbounded transport costs on separable Hilbert spaces. Our framework provides, in particular, a unified treatment of $2$-quantum Wasserstein (QW) barycenters in both the quantum-state and quantum-channel formulations by specializing to the canonical quadratic cost operators associated with the $2$-quantum Wasserstein distances of Caglioti--Golse--Mouhot--Paul~\cite{caglioti2021towards,Golse-Mouhot-Paul:2016} and De Palma--Trevisan~\cite{DPaTre19}.
  The central results concern Gaussian input states and, in particular, Gaussian rigidity: whether Gaussian input states force the 2-QW barycenter itself to be Gaussian and uniquely determined when the minimization is taken over all quantum states. We first show that the barycenter problem admits a Gaussian minimizer and reduces to a finite-dimensional convex optimization problem over covariance matrices.
  The main difficulty is that uniqueness of the optimal covariance does not, in general, imply uniqueness of the underlying quantum state. We bridge this gap through a state-reconstruction principle under covariance complementary slackness that upgrades uniqueness of the optimal covariance to uniqueness of the full quantum state, and thereby prove a global rigidity theorem: if at least one Gaussian input is faithful, then the barycenter is unique among all quantum states and is necessarily Gaussian. Faithfulness is sufficient but not necessary: families of pure inputs still determine a unique barycenter, whereas partially pure nonfaithful Gaussian inputs may admit multiple barycenters.

</details>


### [117] [Fourier Symmetrization for Geometric Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01874)
*Letao Wang,Abdel Lisser,Sreejith Sreekumar,Zeno Toffano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过量子傅里叶模型（QFM）的傅里叶表示，研究对称性如何塑造其表达能力并指导模型设计；提出对称化、随机化编码方法，并在量子物理信息神经网络（QPINN）基准上取得最低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何量子机器学习将对称性引入量子模型，但对称性如何影响模型表达能力、以及如何指导有效模型设计仍缺乏充分理解。本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 采用QFM的傅里叶表示分析：对称性将频谱组织为轨道，并把同一轨道内的傅里叶系数求和为对称化系数；当可训练层构成独立的精确2-design时，推导对称化系数方差等于其轨道内系数方差之和；对单层QFM（ε-近似2-design可训练层）给出偏离该恒等式的界限；以超八面体群举例说明轨道增长可缓解对称化系数的表达力消失；对基本阿贝尔2-群对称化得到纯多元切比雪夫多项式基函数；提出随机化编码，在无辅助量子比特、无量子twirling额外电路深度下实现不变模型。

Result: 单层QFM的逐傅里叶系数界可指数级优于已有界；超八面体群展示轨道增长可缓解对称化系数表达力消失；基本阿贝尔2-群对称化导出纯多元切比雪夫多项式基；在二维屏蔽泊松方程与定常粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程两个QPINN基准上，硬边界约束下，精确对称化与随机化编码分别取得最低平均误差。

Conclusion: 对称性通过轨道结构与对称化系数塑造QFM表达力，2-design方差恒等式与偏差界提供了理论刻画；随机化编码为不变模型提供高效实现；QPINN实验验证了精确对称化与随机化编码的有效性。

Abstract: Geometric quantum machine learning incorporates symmetry into quantum models, but how symmetry shapes their expressivity and guides effective model design remains insufficiently understood. We address this question through the Fourier representation of quantum Fourier models (QFMs). Symmetry organizes the frequency spectrum into orbits and sums the Fourier coefficients within each orbit into a symmetrized coefficient. When QFM trainable layers form independent exact 2-designs, the variance of each symmetrized coefficient equals the sum of the coefficient variances in its orbit. For single-layer QFMs with $\varepsilon$-approximate 2-design trainable layers, we bound the deviation from this identity. The resulting bound for individual Fourier coefficients can be exponentially tighter than an existing bound. The hyperoctahedral group provides an example of orbit growth that can mitigate vanishing expressivity of the symmetrized coefficients. Symmetrization of QFMs over an elementary abelian 2-group also yields pure multivariate Chebyshev polynomial basis functions. We introduce randomized encoding, which implements invariant models without ancilla qubits or the additional circuit depth for quantum twirling. We evaluate the models as quantum physics-informed neural networks (QPINNs) on two-dimensional screened Poisson and stationary viscous Hamilton-Jacobi equations. Under hard boundary constraints, QPINNs using exact symmetrization and randomized encoding achieve the lowest mean errors in the two benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [118] [Floquet-Universal Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01878)
*Emilio Onorati,Harriet Apel,Michael M. Wolf,Toby Cubitt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了 Floquet 模拟理论：用周期性驱动的哈密顿量合成时间无关哈密顿量，给出了 Floquet 通用哈密顿量的完备构造性刻画，并导出若干复杂度理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类比模拟与数字模拟是量子系统相互复制的两种主要途径，分别对应时间无关哈密顿量与完全可控的时间相关量子电路两种动力学机制。时间周期哈密顿量处于二者之间的中间机制，但此前缺乏系统的模拟理论，也缺少对 Floquet 通用性的完整刻画。

Method: 利用周期性驱动哈密顿量合成时间无关哈密顿量，建立 Floquet 模拟理论。证明相互作用集合 S 可在李代数 Lie(S) 中 Floquet 模拟任意哈密顿量，从而给出 Floquet 通用哈密顿量的完备且构造性刻画；构造仅使用 O(1) 阶局部相互作用强度及其比值，所需驱动频率范围对 k-local 格点哈密顿量等重要类别随系统规模多项式增长。

Result: 给出 Floquet 通用哈密顿量的完备构造性刻画，构造仅需 O(1) 局部相互作用强度，避免了时间无关哈密顿量类比模拟中 impractical 的多尺度局部强度要求；驱动频率数量对 k-local 格点等类别随系统规模呈多项式增长。可直接导出 Floquet 物理中自然物理性质与量的多项 BQP-完备性与 QMA-难结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Floquet 模拟建立了统一理论框架，连接类比模拟与数字模拟，并给出可实现的构造方案；其复杂度理论推论进一步揭示了 Floquet 物理中物理量计算的固有难度。

Abstract: Analogue and digital Hamiltonian simulation represent two distinct approaches to making one quantum system replicate the physical properties of another, with consequences ranging from practical applications to complexity theory and quantum gravity. They are rooted in two distinct regimes of quantum dynamics: time-independent Hamiltonians for the former; fully controllable time-dependent quantum circuits in the latter. Time-periodic Hamiltonians represent an intermediate regime. In this work, we establish a theory of Floquet simulation where we use periodically driven Hamiltonians to synthesise time-independent ones.
  We show that a set of interactions $S$ can Floquet-simulate every Hamiltonian in the Lie algebra $\mathrm{Lie}(S)$, and consequently we give a complete and constructive characterisation of Floquet-universal Hamiltonians that are able to produce any target Hamiltonian. Notably, our construction uses only $O(1)$ local interaction strengths and ratios thereof, avoiding the impractical multi-scale local interaction strengths often required in analogue simulation via time-independent Hamiltonians. Instead, the construction requires a range of driving frequencies which scales polynomially with system size for important classes such as $k$-local lattice Hamiltonians. This has immediate complexity-theoretic implications, including numerous BQP-completeness and QMA-hardness results for natural physical properties and quantities studied in Floquet physics.

</details>


### [119] [Exponential quantum advantages for decoded quantum interferometry in the streaming setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01902)
*Kewen Wu,Guangxu Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究解码量子干涉（DQI）在流式模型下的空间复杂度优势，通过Hermite插值与Hasse导数推广最优多项式交集（OPI）问题，证明量子算法以单遍、多对数空间实现93%约束满足，而任何经典算法即使多遍且无限时间，要达到76%也需多项式空间，从而给出参数权衡曲线并证实DQI在原始OPI上的可证明量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI（Jordan等，Nature 2025）在时间维度上对OPI有指数级量子优势，但空间（内存）作为另一核心资源尚未被探讨。尤其逻辑量子比特的容错开销巨大，存储与操作大规模输入极具挑战，因此需要回答：DQI是否在内存上也有量子优势，且能否无条件证明这一优势。

Method: 在流式（streaming）设定下，考虑OPI的自然推广：用Hermite插值与Hasse导数定义问题，要求寻找一个低次多项式，尽可能多地满足关于其取值与导数的约束。对DQI算法进行适配以适用于该流式模型。

Result: 量子效率：改编的DQI算法可产生满足93%约束的多项式，且仅需单遍读取输入流、使用多对数空间、每条流元素的处理时间为多对数。经典硬度：任何经典算法即使允许多项式次数的遍数读取与无限时间，要产生满足76%约束的答案也需要多项式空间。结果给出了可调参数的完整权衡曲线。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了DQI在流式设置下具有可证明的量子内存优势，并由此推出DQI在原始OPI问题上也具备可证明的量子优势，为量子算法在空间资源维度上的优势提供了无条件证据。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) is a polynomial-time quantum algorithm introduced by Jordan et al. (Nature 2025). For a natural optimization problem, known as optimal polynomial intersection (OPI), it achieves approximation guarantees in regimes where all known classical algorithms require exponential time.
  Besides time, space is another central resource: storing and manipulating a massive input can be very challenging, especially when logical qubits carry substantial fault-tolerant implementation overhead. This motivates the following question: does DQI yield quantum advantages in memory, and can we prove it unconditionally?
  We give an affirmative answer to this question in the streaming setting. In particular, we consider a natural generalization of OPI using Hermite interpolation and Hasse derivatives, which asks for a low-degree polynomial satisfying as many constraints on its values and derivatives as possible. As a concrete example, we show
  [Quantum efficiency.] An adaptation of the DQI algorithm produces a polynomial satisfying $93\%$ of the constraints; moreover, it only reads the input stream in one pass, uses polylogarithmic space, and has polylogarithmic computation time per stream entry.
  [Classical hardness.] Any classical algorithm that produces an answer satisfying just $76\%$ of the constraints requires polynomial space, even if it can read the input stream with polynomially many passes and can use unlimited time.
  Our result provides a complete tradeoff curve for the tunable parameters, and implies that DQI has provable quantum advantages for the original OPI problem.

</details>


### [120] [Loss-tolerant distributed lattice surgery using fusion networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01923)
*Felix Burt,Richard Meister,Sheng-Ku Lin,Kuan-Cheng Chen,Michael Hanks,Roberto Bondesan,M. S. Kim,Kin K. Leung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用ZX演算构造混合症状提取协议，将其应用于分布式旋转表面码晶格手术，证明多种协议在无局域噪声时可达50%界面擦除阈值，并分析阈值在截断资源态、局域噪声与融合擦除下的退化及亚阈值性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化物质基量子处理单元是扩展容错量子计算机的可行路径，但跨光子链路需执行分布式逻辑操作。与局域QPU相比，光子损耗和概率性线性光学操作使界面噪声特征不同且更强，因此需要寻找对损耗与概率性操作鲁棒的分布式纠错接口方案。

Method: 采用ZX演算变换构造混合症状提取协议，并将其应用于分布式旋转表面码晶格手术；设计基于融合贝尔对的电路型协议与基于线性簇态的混合协议，通过改变接口几何提升合并观测量与垂直观测量的接口距离，并将擦除阈值转换为光子损耗阈值，结合融合增强分析亚阈值性能。

Result: 在局域噪声缺失时，多种协议达到50%界面擦除阈值；相较直线贝尔对接口几何，混合协议将合并观测量接口距离由d+1提升至2d+1，并将垂直观测量接口距离由⌊(d+1)/2⌋恢复至d。计算了截断资源态下阈值的下降，并刻画资源态错误、局域电路噪声与融合擦除下的可纠正区域。在电路与资源态错误率为10^-3时，局域错误大幅掩盖接口距离优势；当局域噪声降至10^-4及以下时，线性链协议随擦除降低改善更快。

Conclusion: 测量基与融合基量子计算适合作为网络化QPU之间的接口补充电路基纠错，混合协议通过提升接口距离增强鲁棒性；但在10^-3量级局域噪声下接口距离优势被掩盖，仅当局域噪声降至10^-4及以下时接口距离增强才显著发挥效果。

Abstract: Networking matter-based quantum processing units (QPUs) offers a promising route to scaling fault-tolerant quantum computers. This requires distributed logical operations to be performed across photonic links, where noise is characteristically different from and stronger than in local QPUs owing to photon loss and probabilistic linear-optical operations. Measurement- and fusion-based quantum computing are designed to be robust against loss and probabilistic photonic operations, suggesting they could complement circuit-based error correction at the interface between networked QPUs. We use ZX calculus transformations to construct hybrid syndrome extraction protocols and apply them to distributed rotated surface code lattice surgery, demonstrating that several protocols attain a $50\%$ interface-erasure threshold when local noise is absent, including circuit-based lattice surgery using fused Bell pairs and hybrid protocols using linear cluster states. Relative to a straight Bell-pair interface geometry, the hybrid protocols increase the merge-observable interface distance from $d+1$ to $2d+1$ and restore the perpendicular-observable interface distance from $\lfloor(d+1)/2\rfloor$ to $d$. We calculate how the threshold decreases when using truncated resource states and map correctable regions under resource-state errors, local circuit noise, and fusion erasure. We then convert these erasure thresholds into photon-loss thresholds and probe subthreshold performance using fusion boosting. At circuit and resource-state error rates of $10^{-3}$, local errors largely mask the interface-distance advantage. As local noise is reduced to $10^{-4}$ and below, the linear-chain protocols improve more rapidly with decreasing erasure, suggesting that interface distance enhancements are effective in low local noise regimes.

</details>


### [121] [Avoiding Exponentially Large Groups with Open Quantum System Technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01932)
*Jihong Cai,Advith Govindarajan,Marius Junge*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用开放量子系统的完整能力进行态制备：在(n+3)量子比特中识别出一个维度为poly(n)的小李群G，通过反复使用部分迹、环境态制备及群操作，可从任意固定输入态逼近任意量子态；进一步识别单个"开放系统魔"相互作用哈密顿量，实现所有密度矩阵空间上的传递性，并借助更大环境逼近n量子比特上的所有信道，达成信道普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统态制备与量子信道实现通常依赖n量子比特上的整个酉群，代价高昂。作者希望突破这一限制，借助开放量子系统（部分迹、环境态制备、相互作用）而非全酉操作，找到更廉价、维度仅为poly(n)的小李群，从而降低态、密度矩阵和信道制备的成本。

Method: 不再使用n量子比特的完整酉群，而是在(n+3)量子比特中识别一个小李群G，其李代数维度为poly(n)；方法结合部分迹、环境态制备与群操作。进一步给出单个"开放系统魔"相互作用哈密顿量，其酉群可与开放系统量子技术组合，实现对全体密度矩阵空间的传递性；信道情形则通过更大环境实现普适性。所识别的小群来源于局域Pauli串的李代数。

Result: 证明了任意态可从任意固定输入态出发，通过重复使用部分迹、环境态制备与群操作被逼近；单个相互作用哈密顿量配合开放系统技术可在全体密度矩阵空间上实现传递性；n量子比特上的所有信道亦可借助更大环境被逼近，实现信道普适性。由此得到"廉价态/昂贵态、密度与信道"的新分类图景。

Conclusion: 无需全酉群，仅凭维度为poly(n)的局域Pauli串李代数所导出的小群，结合部分迹、环境态制备与单个开放系统魔哈密顿量，即可实现态、密度矩阵与信道的普适制备。这为开放量子系统框架下的普适量子操作提供了新的廉价/昂贵资源分层视角。

Abstract: We propose a novel approach to state preparation by embracing the full power of open quantum systems. Instead of working with the whole unitary group in $n$ qubits, we identify a small Lie group $G$ in $(n+3)$ qubits. The dimension of its Lie algebra is $\text{poly}(n)$. Every state can be approximated starting from any fixed input state by repeatedly using partial trace and state preparation in the environment alongside group operations. Moreover, we also identify a single (open system magic) interaction Hamiltonian whose unitary group can be combined with open system quantum technology mentioned above to achieve transitivity on the space of all densities. Similarly, we show that all channels on $n$ qubits can be approximated by using a larger environment, achieving channel universality. The interesting small groups we identify are derived from Lie algebras of local Pauli strings and lead to a new landscape of cheap and expensive states, densities, and channels.

</details>


### [122] [Optimal query complexity for fractional quantum evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01940)
*Anthony Yuezhang Liu,Adam Wesołowski,Jayne Thompson,Mile Gu,Lirandë Pira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了分数次查询问题（fractional query problem）的查询复杂度下界为 Ω_τ(1/δ · log(1/ε))，与量子奇异值变换（QSVT）给出的上界匹配，从而确定了最优查询复杂度为 Θ_τ(1/δ · log(1/ε))，说明 QSVT 构造是渐近最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给定对未知酉算子 U=e^{iH} 的 oracle 访问，分数次查询问题关注在谱与分支割线间隔为 δ 的前提下，实现非整数幂 U^t=e^{itH}（0<t<1）需要多少次查询。此前 QSVT 仅给出 O(1/δ · log(1/ε)) 的上界，下界未知，因此需要确定该问题的最优查询复杂度并判断 QSVT 是否最优。

Method: 将任意 N 次查询电路归约为用次数不超过 O(N) 的三角多项式逼近 e^{itθ} 的问题，并结合 Remez 不等式导出下界。此外，还给出一种替代性下界证明，通过构造一个湮灭逼近空间的线性泛函，得到对 δ 一致成立的 Ω_τ(log(1/ε)) 下界。

Result: 证明了匹配的下界 Ω_τ(1/δ · log(1/ε))，因此分数次查询问题的最优查询复杂度为 Θ_τ(1/δ · log(1/ε))；替代方法给出对 δ 一致的 Ω_τ(log(1/ε)) 下界。

Conclusion: 已知的 QSVT 构造在渐近意义下是最优的，分数次查询问题的查询复杂度被完全刻画为 Θ_τ(1/δ · log(1/ε))。

Abstract: Given oracle access to an unknown unitary $U=e^{iH}$ , the fractional query problem asks how many queries are required to implement a noninteger power $U^t=e^{itH}$, $0<t<1$, when the spectrum is separated from the branch cut by a gap $δ$. Quantum singular value transformation gives an upper bound of $O\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$ queries for approximation error $\varepsilon$. We prove a matching lower bound for arbitrary query algorithms. Our argument reduces any $N$-query circuit to the approximation of $e^{itθ}$ by a trigonometric polynomial with degree bounded by $O(N)$, together with Remez inequality. This allows us to establish the lower bound of $Ω_τ\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$. Consequently, the optimal query complexity for fractional query problem is $Θ_τ\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$, showing that the known QSVT construction is asymptotically optimal. We also give an alternative lower bound proof based on constructing a linear functional that annihilates the approximant space, yielding a $Ω_τ\!\left(\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$ bound uniform to $δ$.

</details>


### [123] [Universality Sacrifices Reliability in Classical-Quantum Channel Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01941)
*Kaito Watanabe,Masahito Hayashi,Takaya Matsuura,Hao-Chung Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了经典-量子信道中普适编码无法普遍达到信道已知情形下的最优可靠性，揭示了普适性在量子情形下会带来根本性的可靠性代价，并用Petz与sandwiched Rényi散度刻画了两种情形下的最优指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信道的普适编码可同时达到容量与最优高码率可靠性，但经典-量子信道是否具备这一兼容性尚不明确。作者希望探究普适编码在经典-量子信道中能否保持最优可靠性，并刻画可靠性损失的根源。

Method: 通过构造一类经典-量子信道族证明不存在固定编码方案达到信道已知最优可靠性；针对酉不变解码器（普适编码的自然假设）推导可靠性逆界；同时构造信道无关的编码器-解码器对，建立可达界，并在高码率区间证明其与逆界匹配，从而刻画最优普适可靠性；用Petz与sandwiched Rényi散度分别刻画信道已知与普适情形的最优指数。

Result: 存在经典-量子信道族，任何固定编码方案都无法达到信道已知的最优可靠性；酉不变解码器下的逆界可严格小于信道已知最优误差指数；所构造的信道无关编解码方案在高码率区间达到与逆界匹配的可靠性，给出最优普适可靠性的精确刻画。

Conclusion: 经典-量子信道的普适编码无法普遍达到信道已知的最优可靠性，其根源在于输出系统的酉旋转未知。信道已知与普适最优指数分别由Petz与sandwiched Rényi散度支配：对易输出时二者一致，非对易时则不同，这解释了普适性在经典中保持最优可靠性而在量子中会降低可靠性的现象，揭示了普适执行经典-量子信道编码任务的根本可靠性代价。

Abstract: Universal channel coding enables communication without a complete description of the channel. For classical channels, universal codes can attain both capacity and the optimal high-rate reliability. We show that this compatibility fails for classical-quantum channels in general; that is, the optimal reliability in the channel-aware scenario is not always achievable with universal coding due to the ignorance of the unitary rotation of the output system. We exhibit a family of classical-quantum channels for which one cannot achieve the channel-aware optimal reliability by a fixed coding scheme. We further derive a converse bound on the reliability for unitary-invariant decoders, a natural assumption for the universal coding scheme, that can be strictly smaller than the optimal channel-aware error exponent. Conversely, we construct a channel-independent encoder-decoder pair and establish a universally achievable bound on the reliability that matches this converse bound in the high-rate regime, thereby characterizing the optimal universal reliability. Specifically, the channel-aware and universal exponents are governed by the Petz and sandwiched Rényi divergences, respectively. These divergences coincide for commuting outputs but differ for noncommuting ones, explaining why universality preserves optimal reliability classically but can reduce it quantumly. Our results showcase the fundamental reliability cost of performing the classical-quantum channel coding task universally.

</details>


### [124] [Hardware-Efficient Ground-State Preparation using Variational Imaginary-Time Majorana Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01954)
*Federico Santona,Manuel G. Algaba,Aeishah Ameera Anuar,Anna M. Wernbacher,Prachi Sharma,Fedor Šimkovic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出经典预训练算法VIME，将OVQITE扩展到基于单一Majorana传播代理图的分子电子结构，并结合压缩tiled Unitary Product State（c-tUPS）拟设，实现资源高效的分子基态制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期容错量子化学需要紧凑的基态制备电路，以减少经典模拟与量子态制备的资源开销。

Method: VIME（Variational Imaginary-time Majorana Evolution）是一种经典预训练算法，将operator-projected variational quantum imaginary-time evolution（OVQITE）扩展到分子电子结构，使用单一编译的Majorana-propagation（MP）代理图；并搭配新开发的压缩tiled Unitary Product State（c-tUPS）拟设以降低经典模拟和量子态制备成本。

Result: 在强关联钌配合物TLD-1411中，最多52量子比特的活性空间计算达到化学精度，能量误差比基于ADAPT-VQE的ADAPT-VMPE小约400倍；在至并七苯（60量子比特）的强多参考acene系列中，VIME达到或优于ADAPT-VMPE的MP能量精度，同时CNOT数最多减少26倍，双量子比特深度最多降低167倍。

Conclusion: 虚时经典预训练压缩变分拟设是通向资源高效分子基态制备的有前景路径。

Abstract: Compact ground-state preparation circuits are essential for early fault-tolerant quantum chemistry. We introduce Variational Imaginary-time Majorana Evolution (VIME), a classical pre-training algorithm that extends operator-projected variational quantum imaginary-time evolution (OVQITE) to molecular electronic structure using a single compiled Majorana-propagation (MP) surrogate graph. We pair VIME with a compressed tiled Unitary Product State (c-tUPS) ansatz that we develop to reduce both classical simulation and quantum state-preparation costs. In the strongly correlated ruthenium complex TLD-1411, active-space calculations requiring up to 52 qubits achieve chemical precision against DMRG reference energies, with energy errors approximately $400\times$ smaller than those from ADAPT-VQE-based variational Majorana-propagation (ADAPT-VMPE). Across the strongly multireference acene series up to heptacene (60 qubits), VIME matches or improves on the MP-reported energy accuracy of the ADAPT-VMPE comparison points with up to $26\times$ fewer CNOTs and $167\times$ lower two-qubit depth. These results establish imaginary-time classical pre-training of compressed variational ansätze as a promising route to resource-efficient molecular ground-state preparation.

</details>


### [125] [Fully tolerant product state testing and closest product state learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01979)
*Zongbo Bao,Jonas Helsen,Tuyen Nguyen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究判断未知 $n$-qudit 态是否为乘积态的问题，通过随机染色与分块谱投影技术，给出无需依赖 $n$ 的副本数的高效容错测试算法，并显著改进最近乘积态学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断未知量子态是否接近乘积态或远离任何乘积态是量子态测试中的基础问题。现有方法通常在副本复杂度上依赖系统规模 $n$，且乘积态学习所需副本数较多，因此需要更高效、副本数与 $n$ 无关的容错测试与学习算法。

Method: 1. 随机染色论证：对任意未知态，总存在 $[n]$ 到 $q$ 部分的划分，使该划分下与最近乘积态重叠的平方仅比最优值多 $O(1/q)$ 的加性因子，从而将问题归约为 $q$ 方容错测试。2. 结合分块谱投影论证，证明 Harrow & Montanaro 乘积态测试的自然 $k$ 副本推广是高效容错测试器。3. 学习方面，使用随机染色与分块谱投影，结合 qudit 版 Bakshi 等高保真乘积态学习算法，以及基于 Werner 最优克隆信道的采样技术。

Result: 给出了时间高效且副本数与 $n$ 无关的容错测试算法；学习算法使用 $\widetilde{O}((nd)^2)2^{\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^8)}$ 个未知态副本，输出 $\varepsilon$-近似最优乘积态，较已有算法显著改进。

Conclusion: 随机染色与分块谱投影是统一且有效的技术工具，可同时用于乘积态容错测试与最近乘积态学习，并得到高效、副本数受控的算法。

Abstract: We address the problem of testing whether an unknown $n$-qudit state $ρ$ is $a$-close to a product state or $b$-far away from any product state, as measured in terms of state overlap. We provide a time-efficient algorithm to solve this problem that requires an $n$-independent number of copies of the unknown state. Our random coloring argument shows that for any unknown state there always exists a partition of $[n]$ into $q$ parts such that the square of the overlap with the closest product state with respect to this partition is only an additive factor $O(1/q)$ larger. This reduces the problem to tolerant testing of $q$ parties, with potentially growing local dimensions. Combining this insight with blockwise spectral projection arguments we can show that the natural $k$-copy generalization of Harrow & Montanaro's product state test provides an efficient tolerant tester.
  We use the same random coloring and blockwise spectral projection techniques to obtain a substantially improved algorithm for closest product state learning. In particular we give an algorithm that takes in $\widetilde{O}\big((nd)^2\big)\, 2^{\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^8)}$ copies of the unknown state and produces an $\varepsilon$-approximately optimal product state. The key technical components of this learner are a qudit variant of the Bakshi et al. high-fidelity product state learning algorithm and a sampling technique based on Werner's optimal cloning channel.

</details>


### [126] [On the Metastability of the Mean-Field Interchange Model for Local Dimension $\geq 3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01987)
*Sergio Escobar,Lin Lin,Michael Ragone,Kevin D. Stubbs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究平均场交换模型在局域维度 $d\geq 3$、单格点广义泡利耦合下的Davies动力学，揭示存在一个由两个逆温度 $β_{\mathrm{min}}(d)$ 与 $β=d$ 界定的区间，其中谱隙随系统尺寸指数衰减（亚稳态），而区间外谱隙仅有系统尺寸的逆多项式下界；证明借助Schur-Weyl对偶将Davies生成元分解为杨图上的经典马尔可夫链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温量子系统常因亚稳态而热化缓慢，但已有迹象表明进一步降温反而可能恢复多项式时间热化。本文旨在给出这一"降温加速热化"现象的具体可证模型，并刻画其动力学相图与谱隙结构。

Method: 针对平均场交换模型的Davies动力学，利用Schur-Weyl对偶将Davies生成元按对称性分解：一部分化为杨图上的经典马尔可夫生成元，另一部分限制在其余对称扇区并证明其谱隙为 $Ω(n^{-1})$。通过分析杨图链的谱性质并结合静态一阶相变位置，界定动力学谱隙随尺寸 $n$ 的行为。

Result: 动力学相图由两个逆温度 $β_{\mathrm{min}}(d)$ 与 $β=d$ 划分，静态一阶相变恰位于二者之间。在区间 $(β_{\mathrm{min}}(d),d)$ 之外（低温和高温区），Davies生成元谱隙有系统尺寸 $n$ 的逆多项式下界；而在该区间内，谱隙随 $n$ 指数衰减，表现为亚稳态。

Conclusion: 指数级热化减慢完全源自杨图链的经典动力学；移除该链后其余对称扇区不构成瓶颈，说明亚稳态的量子起源可由经典杨图链捕获。这为低温下"进一步冷却恢复多项式热化"提供了可证实例与定量相图。

Abstract: Many quantum systems are believed to thermalize slowly at low temperatures due to the existence of metastable phases. However, there are cases where further cooling can restore polynomial-time thermalization: we demonstrate that the Davies dynamics of the mean-field interchange model with local dimension $d\geq 3$ and single-site generalized Pauli couplings exhibits this behavior. The dynamical phase diagram is beholden to two distinguished inverse temperatures, $β_{\mathrm{min}}(d)$ and $β=d$, with the static first-order phase transition lying between them. At low and high temperatures surrounding the interval $(β_{\mathrm{min}}(d),d)$, the spectral gap of the Davies generator is bounded below by an inverse polynomial in system size $n$. Meanwhile, in this interval, the gap vanishes exponentially in $n$, indicating metastability. The proof uses Schur-Weyl duality to decompose the Davies generator into a classical Markov generator on Young diagrams, and the restriction to the remaining symmetry sectors whose gap is $Ω(n^{-1})$. Thus, exponential slowdown arises entirely from the Young diagram chain.

</details>


### [127] [Geometry of Knill-Laflamme Coefficients for Pauli Error Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01992)
*Baisong Sun,Ningping Cao,Yiu Tung Poon,Bei Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Knill-Laflamme系数的几何结构，证明当算子表示的多重度足够大时，活跃期望数据可提升为精确标量压缩，从而使系数范围成为凸的活跃期望体，其签名谱为闭区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Knill-Laflamme系数通过误差算子到码空间的标量压缩刻画精确量子纠错。给定Pauli可观测量与固定码维数，可达系数向量构成联合高阶数值域，其欧氏范数像为签名谱。作者希望理解算子表示、码维数与检测约束如何支配这一几何。

Method: 主要定理以表示多重度为核心条件：只要每个被占据块的多重度至少等于码维数乘以相应活跃态的秩，就存在提升，使活跃期望数据实现为精确标量压缩。据此分析系数范围的凸性与连通性，并统一处理交换Pauli族（公共本征空间简并产生多胞体）与子系统稳定子码（受保护逻辑子系统提供多重度空间）。结合父哈密顿量构造与三量子比特实例进行验证。

Result: 当多重度足以实现所有活跃态时，系数范围为完整活跃期望体，因而是凸的、连通的，签名谱为闭区间。显式例子显示：增大码维数可使范围收缩、塌缩或变空；增大多重度可将Bloch球变为实心球。子系统设定下，父哈密顿量构造将精确检测码实现为简并基态。三量子比特例子中，额外的非对易检测约束把四面体范围约化为其重心与四个顶点，产生不连通的签名谱。

Conclusion: 本文给出Knill-Laflamme系数几何的结构性框架，揭示了多重度作为可实现性的充分条件，并为进一步刻画结构化误差模型下系数范围连通性与区间签名谱的更精细判据提供了动机。

Abstract: Knill-Laflamme coefficients characterize exact quantum error detection through scalar compressions of error operators to the code space. For prescribed Pauli observables and a fixed code dimension, attainable coefficient vectors form a joint higher-rank numerical range, whose Euclidean norm image is the signature spectrum. We study how operator representation, code dimension, and detection constraints govern this geometry. Our main theorem identifies representation multiplicity as sufficient to lift active expectation data to exact scalar compressions: a lift exists whenever each occupied block has multiplicity at least the code dimension times the rank of the corresponding active state. When multiplicity suffices to realize all active states, the coefficient range is the full active expectation body and is therefore convex and connected, with a closed interval as its signature spectrum. This framework unifies commuting Pauli families, where sufficient common-eigenspace degeneracy yields polytopes, and subsystem stabilizer codes, where protected logical subsystems supply multiplicity spaces for noncommuting gauge observables. Explicit examples show how increasing the code dimension can make a range shrink, collapse, or become empty, while increasing multiplicity can turn a Bloch sphere into a filled ball. In the subsystem setting, parent-Hamiltonian constructions realize exact detecting codes as degenerate ground spaces, selecting distinguished vectors and continuous paths within these ranges. A final three-qubit example shows additional noncommuting detection constraints reduce a tetrahedral range to its barycenter and four vertices, producing a disconnected signature spectrum. The results provide a structural framework for Knill-Laflamme coefficient geometry and motivate sharper criteria for connected coefficient ranges and interval signature spectra under structured error models.

</details>


### [128] [Operational transformation rates of quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02025)
*Giulia Mazzola,Renato Renner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文放弃iid（独立同分布）假设，引入与张量幂态在操作上不可区分的资源态集合，用de Finetti型论证证明该集合渐近可由"几乎iid"态表示，并定义纠缠代价、纠缠蒸馏等渐近转化率的操作类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常用量子态的张量幂描述多份拷贝，隐含iid假设。但从操作角度看，实验者无法排除未知位置的不完美性或拷贝间关联，因此iid假设先验地不成立，需要放松这一假设。

Method: 通过考虑一族与张量幂态在根本上不可区分（对实验者而言看起来像多份拷贝）的资源态，放松iid假设；利用de Finetti型论证证明该操作集合渐近可表示为几乎iid态；并据此引入渐近转化率的操作类比。

Result: 证明该操作意义上的资源态集合可渐近由几乎iid态表示；成功定义了纠缠代价与纠缠蒸馏等渐近转化率的操作类比；由此引出这些操作速率是否与iid对应量一致的问题。

Conclusion: 在无需iid假设的更一般框架下，张量幂态的操作集合渐近等价于几乎iid态，从而使纠缠代价、蒸馏等速率获得操作定义。核心开放问题是：这些操作类比速率是否等于其iid对应值。

Abstract: Typically, multiple copies of a quantum state are described by a tensor power of that state. However, from an operational point of view, an experimentalist cannot, in general, rule out imperfections at unknown positions or correlations between copies, so assuming independent and identically distributed (iid) copies is not justified a priori. In the following, we relax the iid assumption by considering a whole set of resource states that are fundamentally indistinguishable from a tensor power state, and in that sense look like many copies of the quantum state to the experimentalist. Using a de Finetti-type argument, we show that this operational set of states can asymptotically be represented by almost-iid states. As an application, we introduce operational analogues of asymptotic transformation rates, such as entanglement cost and distillation. This raises the question of whether these rates coincide with their iid counterparts.

</details>


### [129] [(A Variant of) Clifford Circuit Synthesis is NP-Complete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02029)
*Luna Lima Keller,Richard Kueng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究最优Clifford电路合成问题，证明了其一个变体是NP-hard的，给出了与现有启发式算法相匹配的复杂度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优电路合成（寻找给定功能在指定基本门集下的最短深度电路表示）是量子与经典硬件设计的核心问题。经典版本在启发式和严格硬度断言方面已被充分理解，但最优量子电路合成的复杂度理论认识不足。已有针对最优Clifford电路合成的多种启发式（如归约到3-SAT），但缺乏相匹配的硬度结果。

Method: 证明分两部分：(i) 将3-正则图上的3-边着色问题归约到仅涉及CZ门的电路合成问题；(ii) 证明额外的基本Clifford门（最显著的是Hadamard、相位门和CNOT）的可用性无法进一步优化最优电路深度。

Result: 证明了最优Clifford合成的一个变体是NP-hard的，从而与归约到3-SAT等启发式方法形成了匹配的复杂度结果。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对Clifford电路复杂度理论的理解：模拟和等价性检查属于P，而判定一个Clifford酉变换能否在给定深度内实现则是NP完全的。

Abstract: Optimal circuit synthesis is the problem of finding the shortest-depth circuit representation of a given functionality with respect to a pre-specified elementary gate set. This is a central problem in both quantum and classical hardware design. While the classical version is very well understood -- both in terms of heuristics and rigorous hardness assertions -- much less is known about optimal quantum circuit synthesis.
  We focus on optimal Clifford circuit synthesis, for which various heuristics are known, e.g. via reduction to 3-SAT. Our main result supplies a matching hardness result: a variant of optimal Clifford synthesis is NP-hard. The proof proceeds in two parts: (i) reduce 3-edge colorability on 3-regular graphs to a circuit synthesis problem that only involves CZ gates, (ii) prove that the availability of additional elementary Clifford gates -- most notably: Hadamard, phase and CNOT -- cannot lead to further improvements of the optimal circuit depth. Our work sharpens the complexity-theoretic understanding of Clifford circuits: simulation and equivalence checking are in P, whereas deciding whether a Clifford unitary admits an implementation within a prescribed depth is NP-complete.

</details>


### [130] [Sparse Hamiltonian simulation with optimal dependence on the maximum column Euclidean norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02030)
*Zecheng Li,Chunhao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种模拟d-稀疏厄米哈密顿量的量子算法，将Low算法的次多项式开销替换为加法对数精度项，达到最优查询复杂度，并应用于稀疏量子线性系统与黑盒酉算符实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Low的算法[STOC 2019]在稀疏哈密顿量模拟中存在次多项式(subpolynomial)开销，亟需消除该额外开销以达到最优查询复杂度。

Method: 给定最大列欧几里得范数上界Λ（或谱范数上界），采用稀疏预言查询的量子算法进行哈密顿量模拟；并扩展至稀疏量子线性系统求解及黑盒酉算符实现。

Result: tΛ≥1/2时，算子范数误差ε下使用O(tΛ√d+√d log(2/ε))次稀疏预言查询；d>1且tΛ≥log(2/ε)时达到下界；求解稀疏量子线性系统为O(κ√d polylog(κ/ε))次查询；黑盒酉实现为O(√d log(2/ε))次查询。

Conclusion: 该算法消除了次多项式开销，达到最坏情况最优查询界；常数误差下任意N×N酉算符实现需Θ(√N)次查询，解决了Berry和Childs [QIC 2012]提出的开放问题。

Abstract: We give a quantum algorithm for simulating a $d$-sparse Hermitian Hamiltonian $H$, assuming a known upper bound $Λ$ on its maximum column Euclidean norm $\|H\|_{1\to2}$. For $tΛ\ge1/2$, simulation with operator-norm error $ε$ uses \[
  O\!\left(tΛ\sqrt d+\sqrt d\log(2/ε)\right) \] sparse-oracle queries. This removes the subpolynomial overhead in Low's algorithm [STOC 2019], replacing it with an additive logarithmic precision term. For $d>1$ and $tΛ\ge\log(2/ε)$, the bound matches the worst-case lower bound. A known spectral-norm upper bound may also be used in place of $Λ$. The number of 1- and 2-qubit gates is linear in the query scale, up to oracle costs and polynomial overhead in the input bit lengths and logarithmic precision parameters.
  As applications, we obtain $O(κ\sqrt d\,\mathrm{polylog}(κ/ε))$ queries for solving $d$-sparse quantum linear systems with $\|A\|\le1$ and $\|A^{-1}\|\leκ$, under standard sparse and state-preparation access. We also give a gate-efficient implementation of black-box unitaries with at most $d$ nonzero entries per row and column using $O(\sqrt d\log(2/ε))$ queries, given sparse access to the unitary and its adjoint. At constant error, the query bound is optimal and yields $Θ(\sqrt N)$ queries for arbitrary $N\times N$ unitaries, resolving the open question on black-box unitary implementation posed by Berry and Childs [QIC 2012].

</details>


### [131] [Adaptivity is all you need: Optimal stabilizer learning using just single-copy measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02031)
*L. Bittel,J. Eisert,W. Gong,A. A. Mele,L. Schatzki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 自适应单拷贝Clifford测量完全弥合了稳定子态学习的样本复杂度差距，实现Θ(n)最优复杂度，并扩展到测试与低稳定子零化度态学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
