Abstract: The Verwey transition in magnetite is a benchmark electronically driven phase transition that is highly sensitive to lattice imperfections and external perturbations. Doping, deviations from oxygen stoichiometry, and irradiation-induced point defects are known to lower the Verwey transition temperature, $T_{\rm V}$. By contrast, the role of remanent strain fields and extended defect structures generated by uniaxial stress has remained largely unexplored. Here we combine dark-field x-ray microscopy (DFXM) and ac magnetic susceptibility to determine how strain-induced defects affect the Verwey transition in stoichiometric single-crystalline magnetite after uniaxial compression and unloading. The DFXM measurements were performed on two samples compressed along the same $[011]$ direction: loading to $200~\mathrm{MPa}$ generated stable line-like defects and remanent strain fields, whereas loading beyond the fracture threshold produced denser defect networks and mechanical fracture. To test the effect of a different loading geometry, we also measured the ac susceptibility of a sample fractured by compression along $[001]$. Real-space DFXM imaging, lattice-orientation mapping, and residual strain mapping show substantial remanent structural disorder after compression. Nevertheless, neither the transition temperature $T_{\rm V}$ nor the sharpness of the transition changes measurably in any of the compressed or fractured samples. This behavior contrasts with the strong suppression of $T_{\rm V}$ by point-like disorder. Our results show that extended line-like defects and remanent strain fields produced by plastic deformation do not measurably perturb the macroscopic electronic ordering responsible for the Verwey transition, thereby distinguishing the effects of point defects from those of extended slip-related defects in magnetite.

</details>


### [168] [Na$_2$Ir$^\text{IV}$Cl$_6$: The Missing Member of the Perfectly Cubic Vacancy Ordered $A_2$IrCl$_6$ Family, Another Potential $J_{eff}$ = $\frac{1}{2}$ Ground State Candidate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01371)
*Siddhartha Sankar Soren,Aditya Patra,Rounak Das,Ritwik Das,Tanmay Chanda,Jens Buck,Indra Dasgupta,Sugata Ray*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 首次合成并表征了无水Na₂IrCl₆单晶，其具面心立方结构与1.91 K反铁磁转变，打破了此前Na基VODP铱酸盐无法稳定立方反铁磁相的认识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空位有序双钙钛矿（VODP）铱酸盐A₂IrX₆是研究在位库仑排斥、晶体场、自旋轨道耦合以及Heisenberg与Kitaev竞争相互作用的理想平台。然而其精确晶体结构与磁基态长期存在争议，尤其Na基体系表现出反常的水合依赖结构与磁性不确定性，导致具fcc反铁磁性的立方Na₂IrCl₆始终未能稳定获得。

Method: 采用合成手段制备无水Na₂IrCl₆单晶，并通过综合表征（结构与磁性测量）确定其晶体结构与磁有序行为；利用热重分析（TGA）验证是否存在晶格水并评估热稳定性。

Result: 所合成的无水Na₂IrCl₆单晶为面心立方（fcc）结构，反铁磁转变温度T_N = 1.91 K；TGA证实无晶格水且晶体热稳定性可达453 K。该立方相与此前报道的"无水"单斜Na₂IrCl₆形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 该工作凸显了Na基VODP铱酸盐中结构-物性的强关联，为探索潜在的完美J_eff = 1/2八面体及近邻Ir离子对间的强Kitaev相互作用提供了新的研究平台。

Abstract: Vacancy ordered double perovskites (VODPs) have been known for more than 100 years, but their exact crystal structures and magnetic ground state remained controversial till date. Anhydrous VODP iridates ($A_2$Ir$X_6$; $A$ = K$^+$, (NH$_4$)$^+$, Cs$^+$, Rb$^+$, and $X$ = F$^-$, Cl$^-$, Br$^-$), having $face-centered~cubic$ (fcc) antiferromagnetic ground state, offer a chemically well-defined platform to investigate the influence of on-site Coulomb repulsion, crystal field, spin-orbit coupling, and competing Heisenberg and Kitaev interactions. Interestingly, among these, the Na-based VODP iridates seem to possess an unusual hydration dependent structural and magnetic uncertainty. As a result, a cubic Na$_2$IrCl$_6$ system having fcc antiferromagnetism could not be stabilized ever. Here, for the first time, we report the synthesis and comprehensive characterization of anhydrous Na$_2$IrCl$_6$ single crystals having fcc structure with antiferromagnetic transition temperature of 1.91 K. Thermogravimetric analysis establishes the absence of lattice water and also demonstrates thermal stability of the crystals up to 453 K. The cubic phase reported here contrasts with previously described 'anhydrous' monoclinic Na$_2$IrCl$_6$ and highlights the strong structural and physical property relationship in Na-based VODP iridates. This new material offers an exciting playing ground for exploring another potential perfect $J_{eff}$ = $\frac{1}{2}$ octahedra and the consequent strong Kitaev interaction within the nearest-neighbor (nn) pairs of Ir ions.

</details>


### [169] [Room-temperature Magnetoelastic Coupling in UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01551)
*Mingyu Xu,Tomasz Klimczuk,M. Brian Maple,Weiwei Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文报道在铀基金属间化合物 UIr₄Al₁₅ 中于略高于室温处发现巨大磁弹性耦合，通过单晶 X 射线衍射与各向异性磁化率测量证实磁取向与晶格畸变强耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料中电子与结构自由度的耦合是诸多涌现现象的根源，但金属体系的磁弹性耦合通常较弱，且局限于低温或微观尺度，亟需寻找室温附近具有强磁弹耦合的块体材料。

Method: 采用温度依赖的单晶 X 射线衍射，结合各向异性磁化率测量，对 UIr₄Al₁₅ 的晶格参数与特定化学键长在磁转变温区附近的变化进行直接解析。

Result: 尽管无晶体学对称性破缺，晶格参数与部分化学键长在磁转变区附近出现显著异常，显示晶体结构对磁取向具有异常敏感性，表明存在巨大磁弹性耦合。

Conclusion: UIr₄Al₁₅ 是磁性与晶格畸变在室温附近强耦合的稀有平台，为磁响应量子材料及功能化磁传感应用提供了新机遇。

Abstract: The interplay between electronic and structural degrees of freedom underpins many emergent phenomena in quantum materials, yet magnetoelastic coupling in metallic systems is typically weak and confined to low temperatures or microscopic length scales. Here, we report giant magnetoelastic coupling slightly above room temperature in the uranium-based intermetallic compound UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$. Using temperature-dependent single-crystal X-ray diffraction together with anisotropic magnetic susceptibility measurements, we directly resolve subtle but reproducible structural distortions coupled to magnetic alignment. Despite the absence of crystallographic symmetry breaking, pronounced anomalies emerge in lattice parameters and selected chemical bond distances near the magnetic transition region, revealing an unusual sensitivity of the crystal structure to magnetic orientation. The coupling enables direct probing of how atomic distances and local chemical bonding govern the electronic and magnetic states in a bulk intermetallic material. Our results establish UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$ as a rare platform in which magnetism and lattice distortions are strongly intertwined near room temperature, opening new opportunities for magnetically responsive quantum materials and functional magnetic sensing applications.

</details>


### [170] [Mass-asymmetry-controlled exciton dressing and dissociation in a quantum lattice model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01594)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过Holstein-激子模型的精确对角化，研究了极性材料中中性激子与声子耦合的非微扰行为。结果表明：等质量时交换选择规则保护对称激子基态免受声子修饰；质量不对称破坏该规则，激子获得强局域极化子云；解离边界由库仑束缚与极化子稳定的全局能量平衡决定，且几乎与内部修饰无关；保护激子的对称性反而使其失去极化子稳定而更易解离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极性材料中，中性激子通过电子与空穴形变势之和与声子耦合；因电荷中性使总源项消失，弹性激子-声子顶点被电子-空穴干涉所正则化。作者希望厘清这种干涉的非微扰结局，即声子修饰如何影响激子的内部结构与解离行为，并解释质量对称与质量不对称极性半导体中激子谱线尖锐或展宽的差异。

Method: 采用Holstein-激子模型的精确对角化方法，非微扰地处理电子-空穴干涉与声子耦合；通过参数化质量不对称，将其与小平极化子原子极限解耦，从而构建包含内部修饰渡越与解离边界的相图；对等质量情形的交换选择规则进行解析证明并数值验证。

Result: 当电子与空穴质量相等时，若声子源在电子-空穴交换下为奇（中性情形），对称激子基态受精确的交换选择规则保护，免于声子修饰；随质量不对称增大，选择规则被破坏，激子获得强局域极化子云。解离边界由库仑束缚与极化子稳定之间的全局能量平衡设定，几乎不依赖内部修饰。悖论性地，保护激子免于修饰的对称性同时剥夺了其极化子稳定，使其在强耦合下趋于解离。

Conclusion: 激子的内部声子修饰与解离由不同机制控制：前者受电子-空穴交换对称性支配，后者由全局能量平衡决定。该结果在晶格激子-极化子模型框架下得到讨论，并对质量对称与质量不对称极性半导体中激子谱线的尖锐与展宽现象提供了解释性启示。

Abstract: In a polar material, a neutral exciton couples to phonons through the sum of the electron and hole deformation potentials. Because the total source vanishes by charge neutrality, the elastic exciton-phonon vertex is regularized by electron-hole interference. Here we determine the non-perturbative fate of this interference by exact diagonalization of a Holstein-exciton model. By parameterizing the mass asymmetry to decouple it from the small-polaron atomic limit, we map a regime map comprising an internal dressing crossover and a dissociation boundary. We prove analytically and verify numerically that for equal masses, the symmetric exciton ground state is protected from phonon dressing by an exact exchange selection rule, provided the phonon source is odd under electron-hole exchange (the neutral case). As mass asymmetry increases, this selection rule is broken and the exciton acquires a strong local polaronic cloud. We show that the dissociation boundary, conversely, is set by a global energy balance between Coulomb binding and polaronic stabilization, and is nearly independent of the internal dressing. Paradoxically, the very symmetry that protects the exciton from dressing denies it polaronic stabilization, driving it toward dissociation at strong coupling. We discuss these results in the context of lattice exciton-polaron models and their implications for sharp versus broad excitonic lines in mass-symmetric versus mass-asymmetric polar semiconductors.

</details>


### [171] [Why polar excitons stay sharp: parity protection of the center-of-mass recoil channel in exciton-phonon scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01600)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 揭示激子-声子问题中被忽略的质心动量反冲自由度，证明1s→1s反冲通道受电子-空穴干涉控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极性半导体中Fröhlich相互作用是主导的电子-声子耦合，但卤化物钙钛矿等材料的激子共振峰却异常尖锐，标准冻结质心近似无法解释。

Method: 恢复精确的质心反冲，推导动量$q_*=\sqrt{2M_{\rm ex}\hbar\omega_{\rm LO}}/\hbar$处的1s→1s吸收通道；在氢原子型Fröhlich模型内推导线宽与质量不对称度的关系，以及弹性修饰的精确抑制定律$S_X/S_{\rm ind}=\eta^2(6-\eta^2)/5$。

Result: 反冲线宽随质量不对称度消失：GaAs（$\eta=-0.74$）反冲通道活跃（2.2 meV），而FAPbI₃（$\eta=0$，0.00 meV）和MAPbI₃（$\eta=-0.11$，0.12 meV）被干涉压制。钙钛矿观测到的27-40 meV线宽无法由质心反冲解释。

Conclusion: 单一Fröhlich常数$\alpha$不足以作为品质因子；经关联激子投影后，控制参数为质量不对称度$\eta$、$q_*a_X$和Rydberg失谐；钙钛矿线宽需内部态跃迁等非弹性通道，$1s\to np$共振为首要候选。

Abstract: In polar semiconductors the Fröhlich interaction is the dominant electron--phonon coupling, yet excitonic resonances in materials such as halide perovskites remain anomalously sharp. We show that standard frozen-center-of-mass treatments of the exciton--phonon problem miss the decisive kinematic degree of freedom: restoring the exact center-of-mass (COM) recoil reveals a universally open, parameter-free $1s\to1s$ absorption channel at recoil momentum $q_*=\sqrt{2M_{\rm ex}\hbarω_{\rm LO}}/\hbar$, whose rate scales as $N_{\rm LO}(T)$. We prove that this recoil channel is controlled by destructive electron--hole interference: the recoil linewidth vanishes with the mass asymmetry as $γ_{\rm LO}^{\rm recoil}\propto\mathcal{F}_{1s,1s}(q_*)^2$, and the elastic dressing obeys the exact suppression law $S_X/S_{\rm ind}=η^2(6-η^2)/5$ within the hydrogenic Fröhlich model. The theory establishes a hierarchy of scattering regimes. In mass-asymmetric materials (GaAs, $η=-0.74$) the recoil channel is active ($γ_{\rm LO}^{\rm recoil}=2.2$~meV); in mass-symmetric materials (FAPbI$_3$, $η=0$; MAPbI$_3$, $η=-0.11$) it is killed by interference (0.00 and 0.12 meV), showing that the observed 27--40 meV perovskite linewidths cannot be accounted for by COM recoil and therefore require internal-state-changing and other inelastic channels, of which the constructive, $η$-robust $1s\to np$ resonance is the leading candidate within the present model. The Fröhlich constant $α$ alone is therefore insufficient as a figure of merit: after projection onto the correlated exciton, the controlling parameters are $η$, $q_*a_X$, and the Rydberg detuning.

</details>


### [172] [Frustration-induced multiferroicity in hauerite MnS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01990)
*Vilmos Kocsis,Harish K. Singh,Kranthi K. Bestha,Yaqian Guo,Maxim Mostovoy,Jeroen van den Brink,Oleg Janson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: MnS₂是一种磁绝缘体，低温下形成共线反铁磁序，伴随晶胞加倍和反演对称破缺，产生高达360 μC/m²的铁电极化，属于第二类多铁材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究MnS₂中磁序与铁电极化的耦合机制，揭示其作为第二类多铁材料的微观起源。

Method: 通过理论分析（微观电子机制）研究磁有序诱导的S₂二聚体偶极矩，以及时间反演对称性和畴切换行为。

Result: 发现高达360 μC/m²的铁电极化；极化源于邻近Mn位点阻挫磁序诱导的S₂二聚体偶极矩；时间反演对称性保持铁电畴完整；畴切换需克服由磁交换能标度设定的高能垒。

Conclusion: MnS₂是一种第二类多铁材料，其铁电极化由阻挫磁序诱导，且畴切换受磁交换能控制。

Abstract: Pyrite-type mineral hauerite MnS$_2$ is a magnetic insulator with localized $S=\frac52$ moments. Below 48 K, it develops a collinear antiferromagnetic order, accompanied by unit-cell doubling and breaking the inversion symmetry. We demonstrate that the latter gives rise to a ferroelectric polarization of up to 360 $μ$C/m$^2$ rendering MnS$_2$ a type-II multiferroic. Microscopically, the electronic polarization originates from S$_2$ dimers that acquire a dipole moment induced by the frustrated magnetic ordering of neighboring Mn sites. We show that time-reversal symmetry leaves ferroelectric domains intact, and that domain switching necessitates overcoming a high energy barrier set by the magnetic exchange energy scale.

</details>


### [173] [Magnetic Phase Diagrams and Spin Hamiltonian of Monoclinic $α$-RuCl$_3$ from Angle-Dependent Torque Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02006)
*Daniel Antoniou,Danrui Ni,John S. Pearce,Robert J. Cava,Amalia I. Coldea,Radu Coldea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究小尺寸单斜α-RuCl₃单晶（<100 μm）的磁性，发现其低温保持单斜结构、在14 K发生尖锐磁转变，并用高灵敏悬臂力矩测量绘制了三个正交晶面内的磁场相图，与菱方样品显著不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-RuCl₃是自旋-轨道纠缠j_eff=1/2、Kitaev及键依赖各向异性相互作用下的候选非常规磁性材料，但以往研究多集中于会在150 K以下发生单斜→菱方结构相变的大尺寸单晶，其物理与本征单斜相存在差异，因此需要研究不经历该结构相变的小尺寸单晶以澄清其真实磁性行为。

Method: 采用亚100 μm高质量小尺寸单晶（冷却至低温仍为单斜相），利用高灵敏压电悬臂力矩测量，在最高16 T磁场下旋转磁场于三个正交晶面内进行测量；通过连续改变磁场角度与强度，从原始力矩数据及其对角和场强的高阶导数中识别相变，并将结果与计入单斜晶格对称性的平均场计算进行比较。

Result: 小尺寸单晶在低温下保持单斜结构并在14 K显示尖锐磁转变；所获磁相图与菱方样品相比存在诸多差异，包括旋转对称性降低、抑制自发磁序所需磁场显著更高，以及独特的场诱导相；通过力矩数据与平均场计算的直接比较，为相图各区域提出了最小磁结构模型。

Conclusion: 单斜α-RuCl₃的磁相图与本征菱方样品显著不同，表明晶格对称性对磁基态和场诱导相具有关键影响；所提出的最小磁结构模型为理解其非常规磁性提供了依据。

Abstract: The layered honeycomb $α$-RuCl$_3$ has been much explored as a candidate to display unconventional cooperative magnetism between spin-orbit entangled $j_{\rm eff}=1/2$ Ru$^{3+}$ magnetic moments stabilized by strongly-frustrated, Kitaev and other bond-dependent anisotropic interactions. Most studies so far have focused on relatively large single crystals that undergo a structural phase transition from monoclinic to rhombohedral upon cooling below 150 K and a magnetic transition to zigzag order around 7 K. Here we study the magnetism of high-quality, very small (sub 100 $μ$m diameter) single crystals, which remain monoclinic upon cooling to low temperatures and display a sharp magnetic transition at 14 K. Using highly-sensitive piezo-cantilever magnetic torque measurements in fields up to 16 T, we report a comprehensive study of the magnetic phase diagrams for magnetic field rotated in three orthogonal crystallographic planes. Our extensive torque data collected upon varying almost continuously the angular orientation of the field as well as the field magnitude, allows clear detection of phase transitions via anomalies in the raw torque data and its higher order derivatives with respect to both angle and field magnitude. The obtained magnetic phase diagrams show many differences compared to rhombohedral samples, displaying reduced rotational symmetry, substantially higher fields required to suppress the spontaneous magnetic order, and distinct field-induced phases. We provide a direct comparison of the torque data with mean-field calculations for magnetic Hamiltonians appropriate for $α$-RuCl$_3$ allowing for the monoclinic lattice symmetry and propose minimal magnetic structure models for all explored regions of the magnetic phase diagrams.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Hyperbolic lattices with mass disorder: Phases and phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02192)
*Sheersh Sen,Christopher A. Leong,Bitan Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究三种双曲晶格（{10,3}、{8,3}、{8,4}）上最近邻紧束缚模型在质量无序下的电子态与态密度行为，揭示双曲狄拉克液体经历半金属-金属-安德森绝缘体两次相变，另两系统仅出现金属-绝缘体相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲晶格上的最近邻紧束缚模型在零能附近分别呈现无能隙狄拉克液体、费米液体与平带三种不同态密度行为。作者希望厘清质量无序（而非在位势无序）对这些新奇电子态及态密度的影响，并考察其量子相变性质。

Method: 采用核多项式方法（Kernel Polynomial Method, KPM）对Poincaré圆盘上开边界条件下的{10,3}、{8,3}、{8,4}双曲晶格进行大规模数值计算，求取平均态密度与典型态密度，分析质量无序对电子态的作用，并提取相变临界指数。

Result: {10,3}双曲狄拉克液体对弱质量无序稳定，在中等无序下发生半金属-金属量子相变，更强无序下发生安德森金属-绝缘体相变；{8,3}与{8,4}系统仅表现出金属-绝缘体相变。所有相变的临界指数均接近于在位势无序所诱发的相变指数。

Conclusion: 质量无序对双曲晶格电子态的影响依赖于其本征态密度特征：狄拉克液体可经历两次相变，而费米液体与平带系统仅发生一次安德森金属-绝缘体相变；其临界行为与在位势无序情形普适类一致。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor (NN) tight-binding models (TBMs) on plaquette-centered $\{ 10,3\}$ (Schläfli symbol), $\{ 8,3\}$, and $\{ 8,4\}$ hyperbolic lattices on a Poincaré disk with open boundary conditions display a vanishing, a finite, and a diverging density of states (DOS) near zero energy (band center), respectively, yielding a Dirac liquid, a Fermi liquid, and a flat band. Emergent bipartite nature of these lattices within the framework of NN-TBMs, allows us to scrutinize the impact of mass disorder on the electronic states and DOS therein. From extensive numerical calculations of the average and typical DOS using the kernel polynomial method, we show that hyperbolic Dirac liquid remains stable against weak mass disorder, undergoing a semimetal-to-metal quantum phase transition at moderate disorder, followed by an Anderson metal-to-insulator transition at even stronger disorder. The remaining two systems display only the latter transition. Critical exponents near all these transitions are found to be close to the ones mediated by on-site potential disorder.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Effects of on-site Gaussian disorder in the ferromagnetic Blume Capel model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01090)
*Kimberly J. Silverman,Daria Lhommedieu,Alykhan Rajan,Kathryn A. Cooper,Richard T. Scalettar,Eduardo Ibarra-García-Padilla*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 采用MCMC数值研究格点依赖高斯晶体场无序的Blume-Capel模型，给出相变线Tc随无序宽度σ的变化及其对三临界点的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Blume-Capel模型的晶体场D是均匀的，而实际材料中控制空位密度的局域晶体场往往存在空间不均匀性，因此需要考察位点依赖的D_i对相变行为的影响。

Method: 将晶体场改为位点依赖的D_i，服从固定均值D̄、宽度为σ的高斯分布；使用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法计算位移后的相变线Tc(σ)。

Result: 在位点高斯无序下，二阶相变线整体被压低（Tc下降）；结果表明即使σ很大时Tc仍保持有限。无序还显著抑制一阶相变，并在大σ下将其完全消除，同时明显改变三临界点的位置。

Conclusion: 位点依赖的晶体场无序会降低二阶相变温度但不使其消失，并能抑制甚至彻底消除一阶相变和三临界行为。

Abstract: We conduct a numerical study of a generalization of the Blume-Capel model in which the crystal field $D_i$, which controls the local vacancy density, is site dependent rather than uniform. We employ Markov Chain Monte Carlo to compute the shifted phase transition line $T_c$ as a function of the width $σ$ of a gaussian distribution $P(D_i) \sim \exp( -(D_i - \bar{D})^2/2 σ^2)$ at fixed $\bar{D}$. We characterize how the presence of this on-site Gaussian disorder reduces $T_c$ across the second order transition line, and discuss how our results suggest that $T_c$ remains finite even at large values of $σ$. We also determine the effects of disorder on the tricritical point of the conventional, uniform, Blume-Capel model, finding that it significantly suppresses the first-order phase transition, eliminating it entirely at large values of $σ$.

</details>


### [176] [Migration Flows: Population Scaling and Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00192)
*Cook Hyun Kim,Sangjoon Park,Seoyoung Jin,Jaiyong Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过县际迁移解释城际平均流量的标度律与重尾净流量分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城际平均流量随人口呈标度关系，且城际净流量分布呈重尾，需要统一机制解释。

Method: 假设县对净流量为高斯分布，其标准差与方向平均流量成正比；由密度依赖的出发/到达活动确定平均流量标度，并将条件高斯密度对相关分布求平均，用随机模型生成混合所需方差分布。

Result: 在固定相关下县对净流量之和为高斯；对相关分布平均后可产生带高斯截断的中间幂律。

Conclusion: 县际迁移机制可同时解释城际平均流量标度与重尾净流量分布。

Abstract: We show how migration between counties in different cities can explain both the population scaling of mean intercity flows and heavy-tailed intercity net-flow distributions. Density-dependent departure and arrival activities determine how mean flows scale with the populations of the origin and destination cities. We assume Gaussian county-pair net flows with standard deviations proportional to their mean directional flows. At a fixed correlation between county-pair net flows, their sum is Gaussian. Averaging this conditional Gaussian density over the correlation distribution can produce an intermediate power law with a Gaussian cutoff. We also present a stochastic model that generates the variance distribution used in this mixture.

</details>


### [177] [Active-Matter Battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00428)
*Yun-Qian Lin,Rui-Han Liu,Yu-Han Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从活性物质中提取能量的极简电池模型，并解析推导其充放电特性；当计入维持活性的能量成本时，充电功率—效率权衡呈环状，效率存在有限时间极大值，与被动能量器件形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用活性物质驱动机能器件日益受到关注，但尚缺乏描述从活性物质中储能过程的最小理论模型，尤其缺乏对效率与功率关系的定量刻画。

Method: 构建极简电池模型以储存在活性物质中提取的能量，解析求解其充电与放电特性，并将维持活性的能量成本纳入能量平衡分析。

Result: 解析得到充放电特性曲线；在考虑活性维持成本后，充电功率与效率之间的权衡关系呈环状（loop-like），效率出现有限时间极大值。

Conclusion: 活性物质能量器件在效率—功率行为上本质区别于被动器件，其效率可在有限充电时间处达到最优，为活性物质供能器件的设计提供理论依据。

Abstract: Harnessing active matter to power functional devices has attracted growing interest. We propose a minimal battery model for storing energy extracted from active matter and derive its charging and discharging characteristics analytically. When the energetic cost of sustaining activity is included, the charging power-efficiency trade-off becomes loop-like and the efficiency attains a finite-time maximum, in sharp contrast to passive energy devices.

</details>


### [178] [Generative Modeling of Stochastic Dynamics for Long-Time Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00546)
*Yang-yang Tan,Jinyang Li,Lingxiao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 无需运动方程，仅凭固定短时间间隔的构型对即可预测随机动力学的长期演化；生成式扩散模型学习有限时间转移核并迭代，可复现二维 Model B 的临界标度与 t^{1/3} 粗化，并在驱动胶体实验中据 10 分钟轨迹预测后续 20 分钟的电流与平均通过时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡随机动力学的精确随机方程通常难以获得，因此需要一种不依赖运动方程、仅从短时观测出发预测长期演化的方法。

Method: 利用生成式扩散模型，从固定短时间滞后下的构型对中学习有限时间转移核；通过迭代该核将动力学外推至远超过训练滞后的时间尺度。

Result: 在二维 Model B（守恒序参量的扩散动力学）中，学得的核重现了动态临界标度与自相似 t^{1/3} 粗化，并与直接模拟一致，推广到两倍于最大训练尺寸的格点和训练集外的初始系综；在周期光势中的驱动胶体体系中，10 分钟实测轨迹即可在实验不确定度内预测随后 20 分钟的粒子电流与平均通过时间。

Conclusion: 短时间观测已包含预测长时间涌现非平衡动力学所需的信息。

Abstract: Exact stochastic equations for non-equilibrium dynamics are rarely accessible. We show that the long-time evolution of stochastic dynamics can be predicted from configuration pairs at a fixed short time lag, without knowledge of the equation of motion. Generative diffusion models learn the finite-time transition kernel from these pairs, and iterating it propagates the dynamics far beyond the training lag. For two-dimensional Model B, the diffusive dynamics of a conserved order parameter, the learned kernels reproduce dynamic critical scaling and self-similar $t^{1/3}$ coarsening. Agreement with direct simulations persists on lattices twice the largest training size and for initial ensembles absent from training. For driven colloids in a periodic optical potential, ten minutes of measured trajectories suffice to predict the particle current and mean passage time over the next twenty minutes within experimental uncertainty. Short-time observations thus contain the information needed to predict emergent non-equilibrium dynamics at much longer times.

</details>


### [179] [The Ericson Transition in Time-Reversal Invariant Systems: Symplectically Invariant Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00624)
*Simon Köhnes,Thomas Guhr*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在具有奇自旋的时间反演不变系统中，基于普适Heidelberg方法，作者证明了Ericson区内非对角散射矩阵元与截面的分布，其渐近展开的首阶项分别呈高斯与指数行为，并推导出次阶项且与数值模拟进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Ericson区（重叠共振区）中散射矩阵元与截面的统计分布，对理解量子混沌散射、复合核反应涨落等具有重要意义。作者针对奇自旋、时间反演不变这一特定普适类，希望给出非对角元分布的严格解析刻画及高阶修正。

Method: 采用普适Heidelberg方法（随机矩阵理论框架下的散射矩阵统计描述），对非对角散射矩阵元与截面的分布作渐近展开，推导首阶项与次阶项。

Result: 证明首阶项分别为高斯分布（散射矩阵元）与指数分布（截面）；进一步导出次阶修正项，并与数值模拟结果进行比较验证。

Conclusion: 在奇自旋、时间反演不变系统的Ericson区，非对角散射统计可由渐近展开精确描述，首阶的高斯/指数行为与次阶修正共同构成完整刻画，且与数值模拟一致。

Abstract: We investigate the distributions of off-diagonal scattering matrix elements and cross-sections in the Ericson regime for time-reversal invariant systems with odd spin. Within the universal Heidelberg approach we prove that Gaussian and exponential behavior is found as the leading-order term of an asymptotic expansion of the aforementioned distributions. Furthermore, the subleading-order term is derived and compared with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [180] [CTMRG as a tensor-network message-passing algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00631)
*Illia Lukin,Andrii Sotnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将角转移矩阵方程及角转移矩阵重整化群（CTMRG）重新表述为定义在一般平面图上的局部方程，通过求解消息张量与角矩阵，可为任意连通子图构建MPS环境，在铁磁Ising模型、多种无限晶格（含双曲晶格）及有限图上系统性地优于置信传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统角转移矩阵方法与CTMRG主要针对规则晶格（如方形晶格）定义，难以直接推广到一般平面图，尤其是非均匀或双曲几何结构。作者希望找到一种与图结构无关、仅依赖局部子图的表述，从而统一处理各类平面图。

Method: 将角转移矩阵方程与CTMRG表述为一般平面图上的一组局部方程，仅依赖平面图的局部子图，并保持一致的拼接以描述整体图。求解这些方程得到消息张量与角矩阵，进而用于为任意连通子图构造MPS环境，从而推广了CTMRG的适用范围。

Result: 在铁磁Ising模型上进行测试，涵盖多种无限晶格（包括双曲晶格）以及有限图，结果显示该方法相对置信传播（belief propagation）具有系统性的改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的局部方程框架成功将CTMRG推广至一般平面图，并提供了一种构造MPS环境的通用途径，在数值实验中优于置信传播，为处理复杂几何上的张量网络问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: We formulate the corner transfer matrix equations, and the corner transfer matrix renormalization group built on them, as a set of local equations on general planar graphs. These equations depend only on the local subgraphs of the planar graph and are glued together consistently to describe the whole graph. Solving them yields message tensors and corner matrices, which can be used to construct an MPS environment for any connected subgraph. We test the proposed equations on the ferromagnetic Ising model, on a variety of infinite lattices (including hyperbolic ones) and on finite graphs, and find systematic improvement over belief propagation.

</details>


### [181] [Thermal entanglement transitions from strong $\textrm{SU}(2)$ symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00826)
*Siqi Mo,Ehud Altman,Samuel J. Garratt*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究强SU(2)对称性Gibbs态中的混合态纠缠，对铁磁相互作用的自旋系统证明有限温度有序相变伴随纠缠相变，且铁磁相中两半系统间的可蒸馏纠缠与对数纠缠负度随系统尺寸增长远快于顺磁相（后者按自旋数平方根的对数增长）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强对称性Lindblad方程的稳态可表现出纠缠相变，但混合态纠缠与热相变之间的普遍联系尚不清楚，尤其是铁磁有序相中纠缠度量随系统尺寸的标度行为缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 首先建立混合态纠缠度量（可蒸馏纠缠、对数纠缠负度）与全局SU(2)单态下自旋关联之间的关系；随后提出SU(2)单态热态的 semiclassical 理论，证明在大自旋S极限下全局单态约束简化为对总磁化强度的Gaussian抑制，并导出描述单态子空间自旋关联的场论；结合三维格点模型的Monte Carlo模拟以及一维自旋-1/2系统的精确数值计算进行验证。

Result: 在顺磁相，两半系统间的混合态纠缠随自旋数平方根对数增长；在铁磁相，这些纠缠度量随系统尺寸增长显著加快（参数化更快）。semiclassical 场论成功刻画了低温与高温下多种混合态纠缠探针的行为，数值模拟与解析预测一致，一维精确数值亦证实顺磁相中混合态纠缠随关联长度与系统尺寸的标度关系。

Conclusion: 有限温度有序相变与纠缠相变相联系，强对称Lindblad稳态亦可展现纠缠相变；所发展的单态semiclassical场论为研究混合态纠缠标度提供了通用框架，并揭示了有序相中纠缠增长的增强效应。

Abstract: We study mixed-state entanglement in Gibbs states with strong $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ symmetry, focusing on locally interacting spin systems with ferromagnetic interactions. Our main result is to show that finite-temperature ordering transitions are associated with entanglement transitions, and therefore the steady state of strongly symmetric Lindbladians can exhibit entanglement transitions. While in the paramagnetic phase it is known that the distillable entanglement and logarithmic entanglement negativity between two halves of a large system grow logarithmically with the square root of the number of spins, we show that in ferromagnetic phases these quantities grow parametrically faster with system size. To arrive at this result we first establish relations between these mixed-state entanglement measures and spin correlations in states that are singlets under global $\rm{SU}(2)$ symmetry transformations. We then introduce a semiclassical theory for $\rm{SU}(2)$ singlet thermal states. While the global singlet constraint generally enters this theory as a complicated function of the full semiclassical spin configuration, we show that in large $S$ limit it simplifies drastically to a Gaussian suppression of total magnetization in disordered phases as well as in ordered phases in the vicinity of continuous thermal phase transitions. This leads us to a field theory describing spin correlations in the singlet sector. Using this we determine the behavior of various probes of mixed-state entanglement at low and at high temperatures, supporting our analytical results using numerical Monte Carlo simulations of a three-dimensional lattice realization of our semiclassical theory. We also use exact numerics in one-dimensional spin-$1/2$ systems to confirm our predictions for the scaling of mixed-state entanglement with correlation length and system size in the paramagnetic phase.

</details>


### [182] [Dynamical Critical Properties of the Random-Bond three-state Potts Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01176)
*Zeynep Demir Vatansever,Ulvi Kanbur,Mohammed Sadra Najafi,Muktish Acharyya,Erol Vatansever*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 采用Wolff与Swendsen-Wang团簇算法对二维随机键三态Potts模型的动态临界行为进行大规模Monte Carlo模拟，发现键无序显著削弱临界慢化（z从约0.54降至约0.24），但静态普适类不变，且静态与动态观测量均表现出缺乏自平均性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无序如何影响二维三态Potts模型的临界动力学，检验键随机性是否改变动态临界指数与静态普适类，并考察自平均性问题。

Method: 在无序依赖的临界温度下，使用Wolff和Swendsen-Wang团簇算法进行大规模Monte Carlo模拟，通过有限尺寸标度计算积分与指数自相关时间并提取动态临界指数z；由Wolff团簇尺寸的有限尺寸标度获得γ/ν。

Result: 动态临界指数由纯系统的z≈0.54降至强无序下的z≈0.24；γ/ν≈1.73，与二维三态Potts模型普适类一致；比热与自相关时间等静态和动态观测量均缺乏自平均性。

Conclusion: 键无序强烈影响临界动力学、显著削弱临界慢化，但未改变基础静态普适类；同时体系在静态与动态上均缺乏自平均性。

Abstract: We study the dynamic critical behavior of the two-dimensional random-bond three-state Potts model using large-scale Monte Carlo simulations with the Wolff and Swendsen--Wang cluster algorithms. At the disorder-dependent critical temperature, we compute integrated and exponential autocorrelation times and extract the dynamic critical exponent $z$ via finite-size scaling. Critical slowing down is significantly weakened by bond randomness, with the dynamic exponent decreasing from $z \approx 0.54$ in the pure system to $z \approx 0.24$ at strong disorder. From the finite-size scaling of the Wolff cluster size, we obtain $γ/ν\approx 1.73$, where $γ$ and $ν$ are the susceptibility and correlation-length exponents, respectively, consistent with the universality class of the two-dimensional three-state Potts model. These results indicate that bond randomness strongly affects the critical dynamics but leaves the underlying static universality class unchanged. Furthermore, both static and dynamic observables, including the specific heat and the autocorrelation times, show a lack of self-averaging.

</details>


### [183] [Work fluctuation speed limit in boundary conformal field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01248)
*Shihao Xia,Ahsan Nazir,Harry J. D. Miller*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了边界共形场论描述的量子临界系统中有限时间驱动的根本极限，推导出基于涨落的精确可饱和速度极限，并揭示普适的功-精度-时间权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子临界系统在有限时间驱动下的根本速度极限，理解外部驱动产生的工作涨落如何作为快速控制的资源。

Method: 以边界共形场论（BCFT）为框架，在有限温度、弱驱动条件下，推导精确且可饱和的基于涨落的速度极限，并给出饱和协议，其表达完全由普适标度维度决定。

Result: 所得界限在Kibble-Zurek机制（时间关联强非局域）与绝热机制（线性驱动最优）之间插值；标度维度较小时，增强的时间关联导致显著偏离线性协议，优化优势更大。

Conclusion: 建立了边界临界控制的普适功-精度-时间权衡关系，适用于量子杂质、分数量子霍尔及超导电路等平台。

Abstract: We explore the fundamental limits on finite-time driving in quantum critical systems described by boundary conformal field theory. We show that stochastic work fluctuations arising from external driving are a resource for speedy control, and derive an exact, saturable fluctuation-based speed limit in weakly driven boundary conformal field theories at finite temperature. The bound and saturating protocol can be expressed entirely in terms of the universal scaling dimension, and the result interpolates between the Kibble--Zurek regime,where temporal correlations are strongly nonlocal, and an adiabatic regime where linear driving becomes optimal. For small scaling dimension, the enhanced temporal correlations produce pronounced departures from linear protocols and a larger optimization advantage. These results establish a universal work precision--time tradeoff for boundary-critical control, applicable to quantum impurity, fractional quantum Hall, and superconducting-circuit platforms.

</details>


### [184] [On thermalization in random free fermions: Statistical origins and energy-dependent ETH structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01414)
*Xiao-Kan Guo,Zhiqiang Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过随机矩阵分析研究随机自由费米子模型的热化，推导热化函数及其渐近衰减，证明涨落方差在热力学极限下消失，并给出本征态统计的因子化定理与相关结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究随机自由费米子模型中的热化行为，并将其作为弱本征态热化假设（ETH）型自平均的可解析实现，同时为分离本征基混沌与谱混沌提供可控平台。

Method: 采用详细的随机矩阵分析；计算单粒子可观测量A下Tr(Aρ(t))的系综平均与涨落；引入粒子数守恒，研究能量壳本征态统计并证明因子化定理；考察受控本征矢-本征值关联变形；计算关联函数涨落并与完全混沌系统对比。

Result: 得到热化函数g²(t/τ_λ)，其中τ_λ=ħ/(2η√N)，证明其|t|⁻³渐近衰减；涨落方差在热力学极限下以O(1/N)消失；证明本征态统计的因子化定理；在关联变形下，对角能量分辨斜率与离对角ETH谱函数呈现能量依赖；关联函数涨落尺度与完全混沌系统存在定量差异，根源于本征态的高斯性质。

Conclusion: 随机自由费米子模型是弱ETH型自平均的可解析实现，也是可将本征基混沌与谱混沌分离的可控设定。

Abstract: We study the thermalization in the random free fermion model by a detailed random-matrix analysis. By computing the ensemble average and fluctuations of $\operatorname{Tr}(Aρ(t))$ for a single-particle observable $A$, we derive the thermalization function $g^2(t/τ_λ)$ with $τ_λ= \hbar/(2η\sqrt{N})$, prove its $|t|^{-3}$ asymptotic decay, and show that the variance of the fluctuations vanishes as $O(1/N)$ in the thermodynamic limit.Particle number conservation is also incorporated into the model, by which we further study the energy-shell eigenstate statistics and prove a factorization theorem of the eigenstate statistics. We further show that under a controlled eigenvector-eigenvalue correlation deformation, the diagonal energy-resolved slope and the off-diagonal eigenstate thermalization hypothesis spectral function acquire an energy dependence.Finally, we compute the fluctuations of correlation functions and compare the fluctuation scales with those of a fully chaotic system, revealing quantitative differences rooted in the Gaussian nature of the eigenstates.Our work establishes the random free fermion model as an analytically solvable realization of weak eigenstate thermalization hypothesis type self-averaging, and as a controllable setting in which eigenbasis chaos can be separated from spectral chaos.

</details>


### [185] [Learning ab initio phase-field models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01432)
*Mengyi Chen,Peichen Zhong,Zihan Zhang,Qianxiao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作提出从第一性原理数据学习相场模型的框架：通过Mori-Zwanzig投影将分子动力学严格降维到物种密度场，用神经网络参数化非局域自由能与迁移率，实现兼具从头算精度与介观尺度的微结构演化模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟微结构演化既需量子力学精度，又需介观尺度覆盖，但现有方法无法兼顾：经典相场模型尺度可达但受唯象自由能与迁移率限制；纯原子模拟精度高却难以扩展尺度。

Method: 将分子动力学在明确假设下通过Mori-Zwanzig投影到物种密度场，导出介观相场方程；其中方程未指定的非局域自由能和迁移率用神经网络参数化，并由高精度机器学习原子间势生成的短时分子动力学轨迹学习；在铁硼熔体与行星条件下氢氦混合物上验证。

Result: 铁硼体系中，模型显示FeB₄成分熔体在常压下发生旋节失稳，而在10 GPa下被稳定，为FeB₄仅能在高压下合成提供热力学解释；氢氦体系中预测了不混溶边界，并在对应220万原子的氦雨模拟中捕捉到液滴成核与生长，尺度远超同精度原子模拟。

Conclusion: 跨成分与条件训练的此类模型有望成为从头算分子动力学的介观对应物，为兼具第一性原理精度与介观尺度的微结构演化模拟提供通用框架。

Abstract: Simulating microstructure evolution requires quantum-mechanical accuracy and mesoscopic reach in length and time scales, a combination that no current method achieves. Classical phase-field models provide this reach, but their accuracy is limited by phenomenological free energies and mobilities. Here we develop a framework for learning ab initio phase-field models, where the mesoscopic equation is not postulated but derived from a Mori-Zwanzig projection of molecular dynamics onto species-density fields under explicit assumptions. The nonlocal free energy and mobility left unspecified by this equation are parametrized by neural networks and learned from short molecular dynamics trajectories generated with machine-learning interatomic potentials of ab initio accuracy. We demonstrate the framework on an iron-boron melt and on hydrogen-helium mixtures under planetary conditions. For iron-boron, the model shows that the melt at the FeB$_4$ composition is spinodally unstable at ambient pressure but stabilized at 10 GPa, offering a thermodynamic rationale for why FeB$_4$ has been synthesized only under high pressure. For hydrogen-helium, the model predicts the immiscibility boundary and captures droplet nucleation and growth in helium-rain simulations of a column corresponding to 2.2 million atoms, far beyond the scale of atomistic modeling at comparable accuracy. Trained across compositions and conditions, such models could provide a mesoscopic counterpart to ab initio molecular dynamics.

</details>


### [186] [Gate Control Improves Routing Efficiency for Periodic Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01534)
*Hyunwook Song,Hoyun Choi,B. Kahng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带门控的两队列方案，用于调节车辆进入并与路由协议结合，可将复杂网络交通流中突发的非连续拥堵相变转变为渐变连续相变，且仅依赖局部队列信息，易于实现和推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于复杂网络交通流优化的研究大多集中于单队列模型，难以应对实际高速公路中周期性大幅波动的车流，且系统失效表现为突发的非连续拥堵相变。作者希望提出更贴近真实交通的两队列机制来改善拥堵特性。

Method: 提出一种简单有效的带门控两队列方案：车辆到达匝道入口时先进入等待队列，待主流动队列排空后才被允许进入，并将该规则与路由协议集成。该策略仅使用局部队列信息，规则简单。

Result: 在高速公路流入量周期性大幅波动的典型情形下，两队列策略尤其具有优势；它改变了系统的失效特征，将突发、非连续的拥堵相变替换为渐变、连续的相变。

Conclusion: 该基于局部队列信息的简单规则易于实现且适用性广，可推广到不同条件下的其他交通网络，或扩展到其他排队系统，如互联网数据包路由。

Abstract: Traffic movement on highways and data packet transmission across the Internet serve as prototypical examples of collective dynamical behavior in complex systems. Although enhancing traffic flow in complex networks has been extensively investigated, most existing studies focus on single-queue models. In this paper, we introduce a simple yet effective two-queue scheme with gate control that regulates vehicle entry and integrates it with the routing protocol. When vehicles arrive at a on-ramp gate, they first join a waiting queue and are permitted to enter the main flowing queue once it has emptied. This two-queue strategy is especially advantageous when highway inflow varies periodically with large fluctuations, as is commonly observed in real traffic. Moreover, it modifies the system's failure characteristics, replacing sudden, discontinuous jamming transitions with gradual, continuous ones. The approach is based on a simple rule using only local queue information, making it easy to implement and widely applicable. Beyond the specific setup studied here, it can be applied to other transportation networks under different conditions or extended to other queuing systems, such as Internet data packet routing.

</details>


### [187] [Optimal sampling strategies in event-chain Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01659)
*James Gulliford,Gareth O. Roberts,Michael F. Faulkner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一个通用框架，将事件链蒙特卡洛（ECMC）中高效应采样策略推广到环面上所有平移对称成对相互作用模型，突破此前仅在特定一维模型实现的 O(N^{3/4}) 效率，并阐明集体粒子动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECMC 相较分子动力学（MD）和哈密顿蒙特卡洛（HMC）等确定性轨迹采样方法，能在不受牛顿动力学约束下推进粒子；已有工作利用这一动力学自由度诱导集体粒子动力学，快速弛豫局域密度涨落，在一维环面 N 个成对相互作用粒子的基础模型中比 MD/HMC 提升 O(N^{3/4})，但在一般吸引/排斥相互作用、低/高平均粒子密度下前置因子过小，实用性差。因此需要一个通用框架来解决适用性问题。

Method: 提出一个通用框架，将高效应采样策略从特定一维模型推广到环面上所有平移对称的成对相互作用模型，并数值阐明集体粒子动力学。

Result: 该框架使高效应采样策略适用于环面上所有平移对称成对模型，有望在计算效率上超越 MD 和 HMC，成为新的最优方法；同时对集体粒子动力学进行了数值阐释。

Conclusion: 该通用框架拓展了 ECMC 的适用范围，具备超越 MD/HMC 成为最优采样方法的潜力，并对物理科学与计算统计具有广泛影响。

Abstract: Event-chain Monte Carlo (ECMC) has revolutionised computational sampling over recent years, providing a powerful alternative to the molecular-dynamics (MD) and Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) algorithms. Each method outperforms the ubiquitous random-walk Metropolis algorithm by advancing particles along deterministic trajectories, but ECMC achieves this without being constrained by Newtonian dynamics. Recent advances exploited this dynamical freedom to induce a collective particle dynamics that relax local density variations on fast timescales. In a foundational model of $N$ pairwise-interacting particles on the 1D torus, this led to an $O(N^{3/4})$ improvement on the industry-leading computational efficiency of MD and HMC - but with an impractically small prefactor at low/high mean particle density for general attractive/repulsive interactions. Here we present a universal framework that generalises this high-efficiency sampling strategy to all translationally symmetric pairwise models on the torus - creating the potential to surpass MD and HMC as the state of the art. We also numerically elucidate the collective particle dynamics and discuss broad impact across the physical sciences and computational statistics.

</details>


### [188] [Scalar field theory for (chiral) active Brownian particles: bottom-up derivation revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01672)
*Yuta Kuroda,Thomas Speck*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过压力展开与重整化群方法，从微观粒子模型（ABP及手性cABP）系统推导含至四阶空间梯度的标量活性场论，并揭示cABP场论中存在两个额外"奇数"项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量活性流体的场论多采用自上而下方式构建，其与粒子模型的联系尚不清晰，尤其缺乏微观推导；本文旨在推进ABP粒子模型与标量活性场论之间的微观联系。

Method: 基于压力展开与奇异摄动理论框架下的重整化群（RG）方法，系统消除快变量并识别慢流形上的动力学，将标量场论作为RG流方程导出；并推广至常数力矩偏置粒子旋转的手性ABP（cABP）模型。

Result: 从二维双体势相互作用的ABP出发，推出包含至四阶空间梯度所有可能项的标量场论；对cABP模型，得到的标量场论除ABP对应项外还包含两个额外的"奇数"项。

Conclusion: 该RG方法为从粒子模型微观推导标量活性场论提供了系统途径，所发展的方法亦可推广用于其他类型活性物质系统的流体力学描述推导。

Abstract: The active Brownian particle (ABP) model is one of the most widely used particle-based models in studies of scalar active fluids. A collection of interacting ABPs is known to exhibit intriguing phenomena that are absent in equilibrium counterparts. A prominent example is spontaneous phase separation, known as motility-induced phase separation (MIPS), which occurs even in the absence of explicit attractive interactions. In parallel, the large-scale behavior of scalar active fluids is often modeled using continuum descriptions, namely, scalar field theories. Such scalar active field theories are usually formulated in a top-down manner, and establishing their connections to particle-based models remains challenging, with these connections not yet fully understood. To advance the microscopic derivation of scalar active field theories, we revisit the connection between ABPs interacting via a two-body potential in two dimensions and scalar field theories that include all possible terms up to fourth order in spatial gradients. Our approach is based on a pressure expansion and the renormalization group (RG) method in the context of singular perturbation theory. The RG method provides a systematic way to eliminate fast variables and identify the dynamics on the slow invariant manifold. Within this framework, scalar field theories can be obtained as RG flow equations. We also apply this method to a chiral variant of the ABP model, the chiral ABP (cABP) model, in which a constant torque biases particle rotation to the left or right. We show that the scalar field theory corresponding to the cABP model contains two ``odd'' terms in addition to those present in the scalar field theory for ABPs. The method developed here may also be useful for deriving hydrodynamic descriptions of other types of active matter systems.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Tunable flat bands and their signatures in electronic specific heat of an Aharonov-Bohm triangular quantum network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00273)
*Sanchita Nandi,Santanu K. Maiti*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了由三角形环构成的紧束缚量子网络的热响应，揭示了平带的形成条件及其在电子比热中的特征信号，并提出AB磁通可作为调控平带位置的手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环状量子网络的电子输运已被广泛研究，但其热响应仍相对缺乏探索。作者希望填补这一空白，并借助热学量寻找识别平带的替代途径。

Method: 构建由有限个三角形plaquette组成的紧束缚量子网络，相邻plaquette通过单键连接，每个三角环穿过AB磁通φ。通过解析推导给出某一能带与电子动量无关（平带）的参数条件，并用电子比热（ESH）分析其热响应。

Result: 发现非均匀格点配位、跳跃不对称与量子干涉的共同作用会产生色散能带与完全平带；导出了使某一能支与动量无关的TB参数解析条件；AB磁通φ可连续调节平带位置；平带位置在电子比热中表现为可辨识的特征峰/结构，从而将平带形成与热响应直接联系起来。

Conclusion: 电子比热可作为识别平带存在的替代手段，并与解析条件和能量色散结果一致；AB磁通可选择性调控电子比热。该方法为识别动量无关能级提供了简单互补途径，并可推广到其他支持平带的简单或复杂量子网络。

Abstract: Quantum networks composed of loop-like structures provide a rich platform for exploring electronic transport phenomena and have been widely studied in various contexts. However, their thermal response remains relatively unexplored, motivating us to investigate it in the present work. We consider a tight-binding (TB) quantum network composed of a finite number of triangular plaquettes, where neighboring plaquettes are connected through single bonds and each triangular loop is threaded by an Aharonov-Bohm (AB) flux $φ$. The interplay of nonuniform site coordination, hopping asymmetry, and quantum interference gives rise to both dispersive and completely flat energy levels. We derive an analytical condition involving the TB parameters under which one of the energy branches becomes independent of the electronic momentum. We further show that the AB flux $φ$ provides a direct means of tuning the position of the flat band. The characteristic features of the energy spectrum associated with this quantum geometry are reflected in the thermal response, which we investigate through the electronic specific heat (ESH). In particular, we demonstrate that the position of the flat band manifests itself as a distinct feature in the ESH, establishing a direct connection between the flat-band formation and the thermal response of the system. Thus, the ESH provides an alternative route for identifying the presence of a flat band, consistent with the analytical condition and the corresponding energy dispersion. Moreover, the AB flux allows the ESH to be selectively regulated through the tuning of the flat-band position. The proposed approach provides a simple complementary means of identifying momentum-independent energy levels and may be extended to other simple and complex quantum networks supporting flat bands.

</details>


### [190] [Interior contacts in a narrow quantum Hall bar: a two-dimensional self-consistent screening calculation of the current distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00326)
*A. Siddiki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文对带两个内部接触的六端GaAs/AlGaAs霍尔条进行完全二维自洽计算，揭示内部接触在ν=2平台随磁场降低经历三种输运区间：欧姆连接、浮动与隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解释Kendirlik等人内部接触实验中观测到的异常输运行为，需在屏蔽理论框架下对包含内部接触及边缘条带的完整器件进行二维自洽建模。

Method: 在屏蔽理论下于全器件平面求解Thomas-Fermi-Poisson方程，计入探针臂与内部接触周围耗尽区；采用填充因子相关的电导张量，通过局域欧姆定律分别对源漏激发与内部接触激发求电流分布，并考察正则化、网格与无序的影响。

Result: 在ν=2平台随磁场下降内部接触依次呈现三种区间：可压缩体区中与电流路径电连接、完全不可压缩体区中浮动于霍尔电势、被封闭不可压缩环隔离；浮动与隔离区间内内部接触间两端电阻上升数个量级，隔离区间位于霍尔平台内并随温度与朗道能级展宽收缩；内部接触与周界探针间电阻趋近量子化霍尔电阻的一半。

Conclusion: 不可压缩区拓扑与隔离区间边界对电导正则化、网格及中等无序不敏感，但隔离电阻大小敏感，表明该现象源于屏蔽与填充因子分布的内禀图像。

Abstract: We present fully two-dimensional self-consistent calculations of the electron density, local filling factor and current distribution in a six-terminal GaAs/AlGaAs Hall bar of width W = 3 micrometers with two additional square contacts, A and B, placed on the channel axis and connected to the outside by air bridges, following the interior-contact experiment of Kendirlik et al. [Nat. Commun. 8, 14082 (2017)]. Within the screening theory of the integer quantized Hall effect, the Thomas-Fermi-Poisson equations are solved on the full device plane, including the probe arms and the depletion regions around the interior contacts, and the current is obtained from a local Ohm's law with a filling-factor dependent conductivity tensor, for source-drain and interior-contact excitation separately. Across the nu = 2 plateau the interior contacts pass through three regimes as the field is lowered: galvanically connected to the current path in a compressible bulk, floating in the Hall potential of a fully incompressible bulk, and isolated from both the bulk and the edge strips by closed incompressible rings encircling each contact. In the floating and isolated regimes the two-terminal resistance between the interior contacts rises by orders of magnitude. The isolation interval lies within the Hall plateau and ends before its low-field edge; its high-field end is sharply set by the disappearance of the incompressible bulk, while its low-field end shrinks with temperature and Landau-level broadening. The resistance between an interior contact and a perimeter probe approaches one half of the quantized Hall resistance across the plateau. The topology of the incompressible regions and the boundaries of the isolation interval are insensitive to the regularization of the local conductivity, to the grid and to moderate disorder, whereas the magnitude of the isolation resistance is not.

</details>


### [191] [From Quantized Hall Plateaus to Topological Surfaces: Quantum Capacitance as a Unifying Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00336)
*A. Siddiki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将量子霍尔效应、拓扑绝缘体与量子电容统一到一个框架中：体拓扑决定受保护的边界态，而量子电容作为静电探测手段可直接测量态密度，从而揭示边界态及体态的自洽屏蔽行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子霍尔效应、拓扑绝缘体和量子电容通常被当作彼此独立的研究主题，但三者存在共同线索：体拓扑决定受保护的边界态，而量子电容是少数能够探测态密度的静电方法之一。作者希望打通这三个领域，说明量子电容并非附属现象，而是观察拓扑所强加的边界态密度的最清晰窗口之一。

Method: 文章采用综述与理论联系相结合的方式：一方面回顾整数量子霍尔效应的量子化边界输运由陈数（Chern number）给出的拓扑起源，及其在Z2拓扑绝缘体零场情形下向螺旋边缘态与狄拉克表面态的推广；另一方面梳理量子电容从二维电子气发展而来，用于测绘量子霍尔效应背后的朗道能级态密度以及拓扑表面态的线性狄拉克态密度。同时引入自洽屏蔽理论，说明量子霍尔体态会分裂为可压缩区与不可压缩区，并随磁场移动，并结合局域量子电容测量加以验证。

Result: 局域量子电容测量证实，在量子化平台区间内体态并非均匀绝缘，尽管平台本身仍保持量子化。自洽屏蔽理论表明体态分裂为可压缩与不可压缩区域，且这些区域随磁场变化而移动。这说明关于量子霍尔效应与拓扑绝缘体之间类比所依赖的“体态绝缘”假设，实际上具有局域性和磁场依赖性。

Conclusion: 量子电容不是这些现象的次要特征，而是洞察拓扑所强加的边界态密度的最清晰窗口之一；它同时揭示了量子霍尔效应与拓扑绝缘体类比背后“体态绝缘”假设的局域、依赖磁场的本质。

Abstract: The quantum Hall effect, topological insulators and quantum capacitance are usually treated as distinct topics, yet they share a common thread. Bulk topology determines a protected boundary state, and quantum capacitance is one of the few electrostatic methods able to detect the density of states. This paper examines that connection. The integer quantum Hall effect showed that quantised boundary transport has a topological origin given by the Chern number, later extended to zero field in Z2 topological insulators with helical edge and Dirac surface states. Quantum capacitance, developed for two-dimensional electron gases, has since become a key technique for mapping the Landau level density of states underlying the quantum Hall effect and the linear Dirac density of states of topological surface states. This picture is complicated by self-consistent screening theory, which shows that the quantum Hall bulk splits into compressible and incompressible regions that shift with field. Local quantum capacitance measurements confirm that the bulk is not uniformly insulating across a quantised plateau, even though the plateau stays quantised. The paper argues that quantum capacitance is not a secondary feature of these phenomena but one of the clearest windows into the boundary density of states that topology imposes, and that it reveals the local, field-dependent nature of the insulating bulk assumption underlying the analogy between the quantum Hall effect and topological insulators.

</details>


### [192] [Strongly coupled ultracompressed self-cavity plasmon polaritons at millikelvin temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00473)
*Luojia Zhang,Andrew T. Pierce,Xuepeng Wang,Simon Reinhardt,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Kin Fai Mak,Jie Shan,Debanjan Chowdhury,Valla Fatemi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出超导自腔结构实现电磁体积压缩约10^7，结合石墨烯层观测到低至0.04 THz的欠阻尼腔等离激元极化激元及其与腔模的反交叉，并开发毫开尔文兼容的亚太赫兹谱仪，为腔-物质混杂系统的谱学与输运同步测量铺路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华异质结具有从亚太赫兹到太赫兹频段的自腔共振，是研究腔修饰物态的诱人途径；但对这类系统进行谱学测量需要在毫开尔文温度下具备高频率分辨率，现有方法极具挑战，因此亟需新平台与测量手段。

Method: 以超导层构建自腔结构，实现约10^7量级的电磁体积压缩；集成石墨烯层以激发腔等离激元极化激元；并采用基于超导电路的毫开尔文兼容亚太赫兹谱仪进行测量。

Result: 观测到频率低至0.04 THz的欠阻尼腔等离激元极化激元，且表现出与腔模的avoided crossings（反交叉）。

Conclusion: 该工作为在毫开尔文温度下对腔-物质混杂系统进行谱学与输运的同步测量开辟了道路。

Abstract: Van der Waals heterostructures exhibit self-cavity resonances from sub-terahertz to terahertz frequencies and thus offer an attractive route toward realizing cavity-modified phases of matter. Spectroscopy of such systems calls for high frequency resolution at millikelvin temperatures, which is extremely challenging with existing methods. Here we demonstrate a new platform for realizing and probing such systems. Superconducting layers define self-cavity structures with electromagnetic volume compression of order $10^7$. With an integrated graphene layer, we observe underdamped cavity plasmon polaritons at frequencies as low as 0.04 THz and which exhibit avoided crossings with cavity modes. To measure such spectra, we deploy a millikelvin-compatible sub-THz spectrometer based on a superconducting circuit. This work paves the way for simultaneous spectroscopy and transport measurements of cavity-matter hybrid systems at millikelvin temperatures.

</details>


### [193] [Algebraic Relaxation of Strong-to-Weak Symmetry-Breaking Order via Charge-Sector Broadening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00474)
*Zi-Xi Fang,Kai Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究强-弱自发对称性破缺(SW-SSB)序在破坏强对称性的Liouvillian动力学下如何衰减：离散Z₂对称下呈指数衰减，连续U(1)对称下呈代数慢弛豫，并揭示两阶段动力学标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SW-SSB是混合量子态的独特现象，但真实开放系统中强对称性宏观上脆弱，可被环境的局域电荷交换显式降为弱对称性。因此需要理解在这种强对称性破坏的Liouvillian动力学下SW-SSB序如何衰减。

Method: 针对离散Z₂对称与连续U(1)对称，引入局域对称性破坏/局域电荷交换的Liouvillian动力学，分析Rényi-1长程关联(含连通关联)的演化；导出U(1)情形下连通Rényi-1长程关联的严格代数下界；在热力学极限下分析两阶段动力学标度；并以费米子链(z=2与z=4)进行数值验证。

Result: 离散Z₂对称下，局域对称性破坏扰动导致Rényi-1长程关联指数衰减，显示离散SW-SSB序的脆弱性；连续U(1)对称下，局域电荷交换拓宽对称荷扇区分布，导致参数上更慢的代数弛豫，并给出连通Rényi-1长程关联的严格代数下界以证明其鲁棒性。热力学极限下呈现两阶段动力学标度：早期R̃₁~t⁻¹，长期R̃₁~t⁻¹ᐟᶻ，z由底层流体动力学决定。费米子链(z=2,4)数值结果与理论预测高度一致。

Conclusion: SW-SSB序在破坏强对称性的开放动力学下，其鲁棒性依赖于对称性类型：离散对称序脆弱(指数衰减)，连续对称序鲁棒(代数慢弛豫)，且长期衰减由流体动力学标度指数z控制，数值验证支持该理论框架。

Abstract: Strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SW-SSB) is a distinctive phenomenon of mixed quantum states, characterized by nonlinear long-range order associated with strong symmetry. In realistic open quantum systems, however, strong symmetry is macroscopically fragile and can be explicitly reduced to weak symmetry through local charge exchange with the environment. In this paper, we investigate how the SW-SSB order decays under such strong-symmetry-breaking Liouvillian dynamics. For discrete $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry, a local symmetry-breaking perturbation drives an exponential decay of the Rényi-1 long-range correlator, revealing the fragility of discrete SW-SSB order. By contrast, for continuous $U(1)$ symmetry, local charge exchange broadens the distribution over symmetry-charge sectors and leads to parametrically slower algebraic relaxation. We establish a rigorous algebraic lower bound on the connected Rényi-1 long-range correlation, demonstrating the robustness of this slow relaxation. In the thermodynamic limit, we further uncover a two-stage dynamical scaling: $\tilde{R}_1\sim t^{-1}$ at early times and $\tilde{R}_1\sim t^{-1/z}$ at long times, with $z$ set by the underlying hydrodynamic dynamics. As a numerical verification, we consider fermionic chains with $z=2$ and $z=4$, which are excellent agreement with the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [194] [How Superradiance Survives Molecular Noise inside Virus Capsids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00739)
*Tianran Li,Irina Tsvetkova,Matthew Brauser,Amala Raj,Philip Shushkov,Bogdan Dragnea*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 将发光有机染料共价结合到二十面体病毒衣壳上，可在室温下自发同步并发出超辐射光爆发；本文实验证实了衣壳存在欠阻尼低频声子样振动，并通过理论建模说明该振动如何促进发射由非相干向合作的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室温超辐射在凝聚态体系中极为罕见，尤其分子体系存在碰撞退相与不均匀展宽，本应抑制相干性。已有假设认为电子-振动耦合可能起关键作用，但病毒衣壳体系中是否存在欠阻尼声子样模式、以及这些模式是否参与超辐射的协同发射转变，尚缺乏实验与理论证据。

Method: 实验上探测标记染料（SR活性）的病毒衣壳中是否存在欠阻尼低频振荡，并观察这些振荡对染料激发态响应的调制；理论上建立模型分析衣壳运动与集体极化、进而与超辐射发射之间的联系。

Result: 具有超辐射活性的染料标记衣壳表现出欠阻尼低频振荡，并能调制染料的激发态响应；理论模型表明这种衣壳运动能够促进超辐射发射所需的集体极化。

Conclusion: 病毒衣壳的欠阻尼低频振动模式可能通过提供动态的相干调制，使染料在室温、存在退相与不均匀展宽的条件下仍能建立集体极化，从而支持自发同步与超辐射爆发；这为分子基低维体系室温超辐射机制提供了实验与理论依据。

Abstract: Excited organic dyes chemically bound to an icosahedral virus shell can spontaneously synchronize and emit a superradiant burst of light. The mechanism that allows it to occur at room temperature, despite collisional dephasing and inhomogeneous broadening, is not understood. Few other known low-dimensional condensed matter systems exhibit room-temperature superradiance (SR). In the fluctuation-prone environment of molecular-based systems, electron--vibrational coupling has been proposed to play a central role. Here, we addressed experimentally the existence of underdamped virus capsid phonon-like modes and theoretically their involvement in the transition from uncorrelated to cooperative emission. We find that SR-active dye-labeled capsids exhibit underdamped low-frequency oscillations that modulate the dye excited-state response, and modeling shows how such capsid motion can promote the collective polarization required for superradiant emission.

</details>


### [195] [Tunable Electron Quantum Optics in Graphene Slit Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00768)
*Mohamed Amine Rhanbouri,Wojciech Julian Pasek,Abdelouahed El Fatimy*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 石墨烯狭缝结可作为实现定向电子束、相干干涉与对称性调控自旋过滤的紧凑几何结构，单层与双层体系均展现出电子光学与自旋相关输运特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一种紧凑、通用的几何结构，同时实现定向电子束形成、相干干涉和自旋过滤等多功能电子光学与自旋输运调控。

Method: 采用原子级紧束缚计算结合非平衡格林函数形式，分析单层石墨烯中三角度翘曲对电子束方向性的影响；通过傅里叶分析与谐波拟合表征磁通依赖的干涉振荡；利用自洽平均场Hubbard计算研究自旋过滤效应；并采用四带连续模型进行谷分辨计算以验证双层石墨烯中的类似束形成。

Result: 在E~2.7eV附近，单层石墨烯的三角度翘曲产生强方向性电子束；边界反射使电子束重定向至公共漏极，透射呈现与h/e阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆周期一致的磁通依赖振荡；局部静电栅可调控干涉相位；本征反铁磁基态具有自旋简并透射，而结构或静电破坏左右对称性可实现自旋过滤；双层石墨烯在E=50meV处展现类似束形成。

Conclusion: 狭缝结是一种实现电子光学与自旋相关输运的通用几何结构，并值得进一步研究低能双层体系的实现。

Abstract: A graphene slit junction is investigated as a compact geometry for directional electron-beam formation, coherent interference, and symmetry-controlled spin filtering. Atomistic tight-binding calculations combined with the nonequilibrium Green's function formalism show that trigonal warping in monolayer graphene produces strongly directional electron beams near E~2.7eV. Boundary reflections redirect these beams toward a common drain, where the transmission exhibits magnetic flux-dependent oscillations consistent with an h/e Aharonov-Bohm period, whose modulation is characterized by Fourier analysis and harmonic fitting. A local electrostatic gate provides additional control of the interference phase. In separate self-consistent mean-field Hubbard calculations, the pristine antiferromagnetic ground state is shown to have spin-degenerate transmission, whereas structural or electrostatic breaking of the left-right symmetry enables spin filtering. Complementary valley-resolved calculations using a four-band continuum model demonstrate analogous beam formation in bilayer graphene at E = 50meV. The results identify the slit junction as a common geometry for electron-optical and spin-dependent transport and motivate further investigation of low-energy bilayer implementations.

</details>


### [196] [Hydrogen plasma-assisted atomic layer epitaxy of superconducting titanium nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00956)
*Yi-Hsun Chen,Yin-Chun Huang,Zachary Degnan,David Sommers,Kaijian Xing,Manjith Bose,Eduardo Solano,David Cortie,Michael Fuhrer,Julian A. Steele,Peter Jacobson,Miin-Jang Chen,Arkady Fedorov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文开发氢等离子体辅助原子层沉积（ALD）方法，在c面蓝宝石上外延生长超导氮化钛（TiN）薄膜，实现了低无序、高结晶质量与超导性能的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALD具有精确、共形的薄膜生长控制能力，是半导体制造的核心工艺，但其在超导量子电路中的应用受限于需要在低温下同时控制结晶度、化学计量比和界面无序。因此需要一种能在保持ALD优势的同时实现外延、低无序超导薄膜的方法。

Method: 采用氢等离子体辅助ALD外延生长超导TiN薄膜，在每个沉积循环中引入氢等离子体步骤调控表面化学，抑制前驱体衍生杂质。利用同步辐射X射线散射表征外延结构与晶体学畴，结合X射线谱学与中子反射测量氧相关无序和氢掺入。

Result: 同步辐射X射线散射显示TiN在c面蓝宝石上呈半共格外延，具有分立晶体学畴和极小的长程结构无序；X射线谱学和中子反射表明薄膜中氧相关无序可忽略且无明显氢掺入。薄膜超导转变温度为2.2 K，动态电感为15 pH/sq，超导相干长度为14.6 nm，与测得的晶体相干长度相当。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了氢等离子体辅助ALD作为结构有序超导TiN薄膜外延的可行路径，并表明ALD的纳米级控制能力可拓展至适用于低温量子技术的材料体系。

Abstract: Atomic layer deposition (ALD) offers precise, conformal control of thin-film growth and is a workhorse for semiconductor manufacturing, but its use in superconducting quantum circuits is constrained by the need to simultaneously control crystallinity, stoichiometry and interfacial disorder at cryogenic temperatures. Here we develop hydrogen plasma assisted ALD to epitaxially grow superconducting titanium nitride (TiN) thin films, using a hydrogen plasma step to modify the surface chemistry to suppress precursor-derived impurities during each deposition cycle. Synchrotron X-ray scattering reveals semi-coherent epitaxy of TiN on c-plane sapphire, with discrete crystallographic domains and minimal long-range structural disorder. Complementary X-ray spectroscopy and neutron reflectometry show negligible oxygen-related disorder throughout the film and no substantial hydrogen incorporation. The resulting TiN films exhibit a superconducting transition at 2.2 K, a kinetic inductance of 15 pH/sq and a superconducting coherence length of 14.6 nm, comparable to the measured crystal coherence length. These results establish hydrogen plasma assisted ALD as a route to structurally ordered superconducting TiN thin film epitaxy and demonstrate that the nanoscale control of ALD can be extended to materials suitable for cryogenic quantum technologies.

</details>


### [197] [Charge and spin dynamics in spintronic THz emitters from Time-Dependent Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01089)
*Ali Kefayati*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文用含时密度泛函理论研究Co/Pt与Co/W双层膜的超快自旋与电荷动力学，发现尽管层间自旋流相似，两者的层间电荷转移、自旋与轨道角动量再分配却显著不同，取决于材料特定的电子相空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁/重金属异质结自旋电子太赫兹发射器是高效宽带辐射源，但超快自旋输运与电荷再分配之间的微观关系尚不清楚，需要从实空间、实时尺度揭示其耦合动力学。

Method: 采用含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT），在真实时间和空间上解析Co/Pt与Co/W双层膜的耦合电荷、自旋与磁化动力学，并进行自旋与轨道分辨的电子结构及相空间分析。

Result: Co/Pt与Co/W的层间自旋流动力学定性相似，但层间电荷转移差异明显：Co/Pt转移较小且部分反向（Pt→Co），Co/W则较大且持续（Co→W）。差异源于基态电子结构和光激发载流子可及的自旋/轨道分辨相空间。两体系均存在自旋轨道介导的再分配与光致跨位点自旋转移，包括重金属向Co的延迟少子自旋转移；层间净自旋角动量转移存在，但无净轨道角动量转移。Co/W中电子密度由间隙态向原子中心态持续再分配，表明光致电子局域化。

Conclusion: 超快自旋输运并不唯一决定自旋电子太赫兹发射器中的电荷或角动量再分配，后者关键依赖材料特定的电子相空间，为理解和设计自旋电子太赫兹源提供了微观依据。

Abstract: Spintronic terahertz (THz) emitters based on ferromagnet/heavy-metal heterostructures provide efficient broadband sources of THz radiation, yet the microscopic relation between ultrafast spin transport and charge redistribution remains incompletely understood. Here, we employ time-dependent density functional theory to resolve the coupled charge, spin, and magnetization dynamics of Co/Pt and Co/W bilayers in real time and space. Despite qualitatively similar interlayer spin-current dynamics, the two heterostructures exhibit markedly different interlayer charge transfer: Co/Pt shows a relatively small and partially reversed transfer from Pt to Co, whereas Co/W exhibits a larger and persistent transfer from Co to W. We trace these differences to the material-dependent ground-state electronic structure and the spin- and orbital-resolved phase space accessible to photoexcited carriers. Spin-resolved dynamics further reveal coexisting spin-orbit-mediated redistribution and optically induced intersite spin transfer, including a delayed minority-spin transfer from the heavy metal to Co common to both systems. While net electronic spin angular momentum is transferred between the layers, we find no corresponding net transfer of electronic orbital angular momentum. In Co/W, the persistent redistribution of electronic density from interstitial to atom-centered states additionally provides strong evidence for light-induced electronic localization. These results demonstrate that ultrafast spin transport does not uniquely determine charge or angular-momentum redistribution in spintronic THz emitters, which instead depends critically on the material-specific electronic phase space.

</details>


### [198] [Gate Dispersive Charge Detection in Ge/SiGe Quantum Dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01100)
*M. R. Tanvir,E. Sajadi,M. Veldhorst,G. Scappucci,J. Salfi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文展示了Ge双量子点及单空穴盒-双量子点系统的紧凑型射频栅极色散电荷读出方法，SNR提升20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ge异质结中横向门控量子点是自旋量子比特的主流方案，但现有RF单空穴晶体管和集成超导谐振器读出方式尺寸较大，限制了可扩展性，因此需要更紧凑的读出方案。

Method: 采用紧凑型RF栅极色散电荷读出技术，对Ge双量子点（DQD）以及单空穴盒（SHB）-DQD系统进行测量，分辨量子点加载与点间跃迁，并进行功率和温度依赖测量。

Result: 在DQD耦合t_c≈5 GHz时，SHB将540 MHz电路的点点间信噪比提升20倍，单位SNR时间从直接探测的2.7 ms降至135 μs；功率和温度依赖测量表明SNR受介电损耗限制，影响阻抗匹配。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了基于栅极色散检测的紧凑读出方案，适用于Ge/SiGe异质结量子点。

Abstract: Spin qubits are an attractive platform for scalable quantum information processing, and recently, laterally gated quantum dots (QDs) in Ge heterostructures have emerged as a leading implementation of spin qubits. To date, RF single-hole transistors (SHT) and integrated superconducting resonators have been used for spin and charge readout, but their size can pose challenges. Here we demonstrate compact RF gate-dispersive charge readout of a Ge double QD (DQD), and a single hole box (SHB)-DQD system. We resolve QD loading and interdot transitions with both techniques. We find that the SHB enhances the interdot signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of our 540 MHz circuit by a factor of 20 for a DQD coupling $t_c \approx 5$ GHz, providing a unity-SNR at 135 microseconds, c.f. for 2.7 miliseconds for the direct detection. Power- and temperature-dependent measurements verify that SNR is limited by dielectric loss which compromises impedance matching. These results establish a compact readout approach based on gate-dispersive detection for Ge/SiGe heterostructure QDs.

</details>


### [199] [Nutational Spin Pumping and Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01164)
*Hans Gløckner Giil,Arne Brataas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在太赫兹驱动频率下，磁化动力学进入惯性区，超越标准LLG理论。作者揭示了一种包含体项和界面项的章动阻尼力矩，界面项产生与驱动频率平方成正比的直流自旋流（章动自旋泵浦），并通过散射方法将效应与微观材料参数关联，预测其在超薄薄膜和低费米能导体中显著增强，可通过逆自旋霍尔效应探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert（LLG）理论在太赫兹驱动频率下失效，磁化动力学进入惯性区，现有理论框架无法描述该区域的新效应，因此需要扩展理论以刻画高频下的阻尼和自旋输运行为。

Method: 采用散射方法（scattering approach），在惯性区建立含章动阻尼力矩的模型，将力矩分解为体项和界面项，并将界面项与微观材料参数（如薄膜厚度、费米能）相联系。

Result: 发现界面项产生章动自旋泵浦——一种与驱动频率平方成正比的直流自旋流；预测该效应在超薄薄膜和低费米能导体中显著增强；可通过逆自旋霍尔效应直接探测，或通过厚度依赖的章动阻尼重正化间接探测。

Conclusion: 章动自旋泵浦是太赫兹惯性磁化动力学中的新效应，其强度对材料和厚度敏感，为高频自旋电子学提供了新的探测手段和调控途径。

Abstract: At terahertz drive frequencies, magnetization dynamics enter an inertial regime beyond the scope of standard Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG) theory. We reveal a nutational damping torque comprising a bulk term and an interfacial contribution that describes spin emission into adjacent metals. The interfacial term generates nutational spin pumping: a dc spin current that scales quadratically with the drive frequency. Using a scattering approach, we relate this effect to microscopic material parameters and predict pronounced enhancements in ultrathin films and low-Fermi-energy conductors. Nutational spin pumping can be detected directly via the inverse spin Hall effect or indirectly via thickness-dependent renormalization of nutational damping.

</details>


### [200] [Synthesis of Metal-Semiconductor Heterojunctions in Single Graphene Nanoribbons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01194)
*Ziyi Wang,Kaitlin Slicker,Weichen Tang,Boyu Qie,Rafal Zuzak,Haowen Pang,Yudi Huang,Xinheng Li,Peter H. Jacobse,Steven G. Louie,Felix R. Fischer,Michael F. Crommie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过表面合成结合STM诱导脱氢，在不改变宽度与碳骨架连接性的前提下，将金属性7-iGNR片段直接写入半导体性H₂-7-iGNR中，实现原子级精确的金属-半导体GNR界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNR是自下而上合成的可调一维量子材料，带隙与带边对齐可控，是有前景的纳米电子学平台，但构建高质量电子界面（尤其是金属-半导体接触）仍然困难。

Method: 采用顺序表面合成与扫描隧道显微镜（STM）诱导脱氢相结合的方法，在半导体性H₂-7-iGNR中直接"写入"金属性7-iGNR片段；利用扫描隧道谱（STS）与第一性原理计算表征电子结构，并用扩展的Su-Schrieffer-Heeger锯齿梯（zigzag-ladder）哈密顿量建模。

Result: 金属性片段在费米能级E_F处呈现非零谱权重和空间扩展的电子态，与两条穿过E_F的色散能带一致；新金属态可由扩展SSH锯齿梯哈密顿量很好描述。相邻相连半导体GNR片段的价带边接近金属片段的E_F，STS在界面两侧约1 nm内即恢复体相GNR性质，无可辨识的耗尽区，带边对齐对应较小的空穴注入势垒。

Conclusion: 该结果为面向未来GNR基电子器件的原子级精确、低势垒p型接触提供了一条可能途径。

Abstract: Graphene nanoribbons (GNRs) are a highly tunable class of one-dimensional (1D) quantum materials that can be fabricated through bottom-up synthesis. Precise control over GNR band gaps and band-edge alignments has established them as a promising nanoelectronics platform, but forming high-quality electronic interfaces remains challenging. Here we combine sequential on-surface synthesis and scanning tunneling microscopy (STM)-induced dehydrogenation to directly write metallic 7-iGNR segments into otherwise semiconducting H$_2$-7-iGNRs without changing the GNR width or carbon backbone connectivity. The resulting metallic segments exhibit nonzero spectral weight at the Fermi level ($E_\mathrm{F}$) and spatially extended electronic states, consistent with two dispersive bands crossing $E_\mathrm{F}$. The new metallic states are well described by an extended Su--Schrieffer--Heeger zigzag-ladder Hamiltonian. Scanning tunnelling spectroscopy (STS) and first-principles calculations show that the valence band edge of adjacent, connected semiconducting GNR segments lies close to $E_\mathrm{F}$ for the metallic segments. STS spectra recover bulk GNR properties within $\sim$1 nm on either side of the metal-semiconductor GNR interface, with no discernible depletion region. The observed GNR band edge alignment is consistent with a small energy barrier for hole injection, thus suggesting a possible route towards atomically precise, low-barrier p-type contacts for future GNR-based electronic devices.

</details>


### [201] [Phonon interference induced by defect pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01200)
*Ran-Bo Yang,Ming-Hao Li,Chun-Yu Cai,Yue-Wen Wang,Zhi-Qing Li,Li-Xia Zhao,Zi-Wu Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出由缺陷对诱导的声子干涉理论模型，揭示黄-里斯因子可定量表征干涉条纹强度，缺陷对内参量可调控干涉图样几何并诱导狄拉克型与节点线拓扑态，且超快光谱与干涉条纹呈"锁-钥"式匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶格波相干动力学对探究非平衡条件下电子-声子耦合的非绝热现象具有重要意义，但缺陷对如何作为相干动力学驱动源、其诱导的声子干涉如何定量表征与调控尚缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 构建缺陷对诱导声子干涉的理论模型：基于强局域电子-声子耦合，由两个缺陷各自独立发射选择性模式的晶格波；引入黄-里斯因子作为可测量量评估干涉条纹强度；通过缺陷对内参量（电荷态、空间距离、能级深度、局域化强度）调控干涉图样；并利用黄-里斯模型模拟缺陷对作为色心被激发时的超快光学光谱。

Result: 干涉条纹强度可由黄-里斯因子直接定量评估；干涉图样几何可通过缺陷对内参量调控；在适当内参量下涌现狄拉克型与节点线等新型拓扑量子态；超快光谱强度与声子干涉条纹完美匹配，呈现"锁-钥"式对应关系。

Conclusion: 缺陷对作为多自由度相干动力学过程的驱动力，为非平衡与超快物理中探索基本量子现象提供了理想平台。

Abstract: The coherent dynamics of lattice waves is of significant importance for exploring the nonadiabatic phenomena of the electron-phonon coupling under nonequilibrium conditions. Here, we propose a theoretical model for phonon interference induced by a defect pair, in which lattice waves with the selective modes are launched independently by each of defects due to the strongly local electron-phonon coupling. We find that the intensities of phonon interference fringes could be directly evaluated by a measurable quantity in experiments--Huang-Rhys factor, and the geometry of interference patterns could be modified by the internal parameters of defect pair, including the charge states, spatial distance, energy-level depth and localization strength. In particular, several novel quantum states, such as the Dirac-like and nodal-line topological states are emerged for the appropriate internal parameters. Furthermore, using Huang-Rhys model, we simulate the ultrafast optical spectroscopy in the phonon interference duration when defect pairs are excited as the color centers, where interference fringes are perfectly matched by intensities of spectrum, similar to the $\textit{lock-key}$ pattern. These results show that defect pair, serving as the driven force for the coherent dynamical processes for multidegrees of freedom, offers an ideal platform to explore fundamental quantum phenomena in nonequilibrium and ultrafast physics.

</details>


### [202] [Composite fermions in ideal Chern bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01217)
*Songyang Pu,Liangtao Peng,Shaffique Adam*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文将复合费米子框架推广至分数陈绝缘体，在Aharonov-Casher能带中构造Jain态及其准粒子波函数，发现其与精确本征态高度重叠，并揭示复合费米子能带结构超越传统有效朗道能级图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合费米子框架成功将强关联分数量子霍尔态映射为约化有效磁场中的弱相互作用整数量子霍尔态，但分数陈绝缘体中均匀磁场被陈能带的非均匀贝里曲率取代，该框架如何被修正尚不清楚。

Method: 在以Aharonov-Casher能带（理想陈能带的常用模型）为背景的体系中，构造Jain态及其准粒子与准空穴的复合费米子波函数，并与精确对角化本征态进行重叠比较。

Result: 所构造波函数在较宽的场调制范围内与精确本征态紧密重叠；涌现的复合费米子能带结构超出传统有效朗道能级图像，能正确刻画准粒子色散；一个孤立准粒子能带携带的总贝里通量与朗道能级不同。

Conclusion: 结果为可移动的分数陈绝缘体任意子建立了复合费米子描述，并提供了理解其稳定性的理论框架。

Abstract: The composite-fermion framework maps the strongly correlated fractional quantum Hall state onto a weakly interacting integer quantum Hall state in a reduced effective magnetic field. We ask how this framework is modified for fractional Chern insulators, where the role of a uniform magnetic field is replaced by the nonuniform Berry curvature of a Chern band. Specifically, we construct composite-fermion wave functions for Jain states and their quasiparticles and quasiholes in Aharonov-Casher bands, a widely used model for ideal Chern bands. We find that these states overlap closely with exact eigenstates over a broad range of field modulation. Using these wave functions, we demonstrate that the emergent composite-fermion band structure goes beyond the conventional picture of effective Landau levels, correctly capturing the quasiparticle dispersion. Remarkably, we find that an isolated quasiparticle band carries a total Berry flux that is different from a Landau level. These results establish a composite-fermion description for mobile fractional Chern insulator anyons and provide a framework to understand their stability.

</details>


### [203] [Inertial Dynamics of a Skymeron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01339)
*Mona Bhukta,Duc Minh Tran,Kilian Leutner,Takaaki Dohi,Nikolai S. Kiselev,Filipp N. Rybakov,Olle Eriksson,Fabian Kammerbauer,Sebastian Wintz,Markus Weigand,Hendrik Ohldag,Maria-Andromachi Syskaki,Robin Tietgen,Edoardo Mangini,Sabrina Kerber,Thibaud Denneulin,Joseph Vimal Vas,Rafal E. Dunin-Borkowski,Bastian Pfau,Oleg A. Tretiakov,Robert Frömter,Mathias Kläui*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过在合成反铁磁体中调控两层有效磁各向异性差，诱导spin-flop转变，使反铁磁斯格明子同伦重构为"skymeron"——层间耦合的斯格明子-双麦纫复合三维自旋织构，并揭示其极性依赖的超快动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁拓扑自旋织构因补偿磁序而抑制斯格明子霍尔效应并具备超快动力学，适合低功耗自旋电子学器件；但本征反铁磁体两子晶格相同，缺乏独立调控自由度。合成反铁磁体可通过独立调节两层铁磁层性质来引入额外调控机制，从而探索传统反铁磁体中不可能出现的复合自旋织构与内部动力学自由度。

Method: 在合成反铁磁体中调节两层的有效磁各向异性差，驱动spin-flop转变（一层转向面外、另一层保持面内），形成正交构型；利用元素分辨X射线显微术逐层解析自旋织构；采用时间分辨泵浦-探测X射线显微术观测电流脉冲下的动力学；结合微磁模拟分析动力学起源。

Result: 反铁磁斯格明子在同伦重构下转变为由面外斯格明子与面内双麦纫耦合构成的复合织构（skymeron），X射线显微术逐层验证：一层为面外斯格明子，另一层为面内双麦纫。电流脉冲下发现独特的极性依赖动力学：脉冲后出现类惯性运动延续，随后较缓慢地回归钉扎初始态；微磁模拟表明类惯性传播源于skymeron重取向的有限弛豫时间。

Conclusion: 层选择性各向异性工程是构建具有内部动力学自由度的新型复合自旋织构的有效途径，这类织构在子晶格相同的传统反铁磁体中无法实现，为低功耗自旋电子学器件提供了新的调控维度。

Abstract: Topological spin textures in antiferromagnets inherit the compensated magnetic order of the host material, resulting for skyrmions in the suppression of the skyrmion Hall effect and enabling ultrafast dynamics that make them attractive for low-power spintronic devices. In intrinsic antiferromagnets, the two sublattices are identical, but synthetic antiferromagnets offer additional control mechanisms by enabling independent tuning of the properties of two ferromagnetic layers. Analogous to a ferrimagnet, the total magnetic moment can be adjusted by compensating the two-layer moments. Here, we demonstrate that tuning the effective magnetic anisotropy difference between the two layers drives a spin-flop transition where one layer reorients and thus stabilizes an orthogonal configuration with one layer oriented along the out-of-plane direction and the other in-plane. In this transition, an antiferromagnetic skyrmion undergoes a homotopic reconfiguration into a complex spin texture comprising a skyrmion coupled to an in-plane bimeron. Element-specific X-ray microscopy resolves this texture layer by layer: one hosts an out-of-plane skyrmion coupled to an in-plane bimeron in the other. We refer to this previously unexplored three-dimensional spin texture as a skymeron. By using time-resolved pump-probe X-ray microscopy, we discover unique polarity ,dependent dynamics during current pulses: a short post-pulse inertia-like continuation of the motion, and a slower return towards the pinned initial state. Micromagnetic simulations reveal that the inertia-like propagation originates from the finite-time relaxation of reorientation of the skymeron. Our results establish layer-selective anisotropy engineering as a route to uncharted composite spin textures with internal dynamical degrees of freedom, not possible in conventional antiferromagnets with identical sublattices.

</details>


### [204] [Transport theory for a generic two-arm co-propagating Majorana interferometer with Majorana fermion and edge vortex tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01402)
*Domenico Giuliano,Andrea Nava,Fabian Hassler,Reinhold Egger*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究近邻耦合量子反常霍尔系统中同向传播的手性马约拉纳边缘模式，通过引入额外复制扇区的玻色化方法，将以往处理推广到完全非对称情形，并分析马赫-曾德尔干涉仪中的边缘涡旋干涉效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共传播手性马约拉纳边缘模式兼具边缘涡旋作为飞行非阿贝尔伊辛任意子的前景与通过任意子融合实现全电学探测的可能，但以往理论处理通常受限于对称性假设，需要推广到更一般的非对称构型。

Method: 对模型引入额外复制扇区进行玻色化，将处理推广至完全非对称构型；分析马赫-曾德尔干涉仪，计算直流电导与低频响应，并考察其与拓扑自旋、非常规共形维数及热长度 $l_T=\hbar v/(\pi k_B T)$ 的关系。

Result: 直流电导不显示边缘涡旋干涉特征；在低频下该干涉表现为有效电容，该电容对边缘涡旋的非阿贝尔任意子统计敏感，并携带其拓扑自旋与非常规共形维数的信号。低温度下相关尺度为热长度 $l_T$；只要干涉仪臂长在该长度尺度内匹配，非阿贝尔统计的输运信号仍可见。

Conclusion: 低频有效电容可作为探测边缘涡旋非阿贝尔任意子统计的输运量，热长度是决定信号可见性的关键尺度。

Abstract: Co-propagating chiral Majorana edge modes are predicted to exist in proximity-coupled quantum anomalous Hall systems. They combine the exciting prospects of edge vortices as flying non-Abelian Ising anyons, with their fully electrical detection through anyon fusion processes. In this setup, electrical transport arises from the interference of a pair of Majorana edge states. By bosonizing the model with an additional replica sector, we generalize previous treatments to fully asymmetric configurations. We analyze a Mach-Zehnder interferometer and show that while the DC conductance does not exhibit signatures of edge-vortex interference, at low frequencies, this interference manifests as an effective capacitance. This quantity is sensitive to the non-Abelian anyon statistics of edge vortices and, in particular, carries signatures of their topological spin and nontrivial conformal dimension. We demonstrate that the thermal length $l_T = \hbar v/(πk_B T)$ is the relevant scale at low temperatures $T$, analogous to quasiparticle transport in conventional quantum Hall edge states with velocity $v$. As long as the interferometer arm lengths match within this length scale, transport signatures of the non-Abelian statistics of edge vortices remain visible.

</details>


### [205] [Sector-Resolved Winding Selection Rules for Structured-Light-Driven dc Currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01423)
*Tomohiro Tamaya,Kenichi L. Ishikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文以石墨烯为平台，从电流算符层面分解局部与梯度扇区，给出了结构光驱动直流电流的方位角绕数选择定则，澄清了绕数既非仅由光的轨道角动量决定，也由电流算符角结构及方位角投影共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构光可在二维材料中产生具有方位角绕数的直流电流，但其绕数阶数 m 的支配规律此前并不清楚；理解并调控该绕数，对基于结构光的电流与磁场定制化控制具有重要意义。

Method: 以石墨烯为洁净二维平台，在电流算符层面将电流响应分解为局部（local）扇区与梯度（gradient）扇区，解析推导各扇区的角结构选择定则，并用数值时间演化计算进行验证。

Result: 绕数由对应电流算符的角结构及沿方位角方向的投影共同决定，而非仅由光学轨道角动量 ℓ 决定。对标量拉盖尔–高斯光束：线偏振下局部扇区为 m=|ℓ±1|，梯度扇区为 m=|ℓ|,|ℓ±2|；圆偏振（螺旋度 σ=±1）下局部扇区为 m=|ℓ−σ|，梯度扇区为 m=|ℓ+2σ|。数值计算验证了上述定则，并表明螺旋度可选出 m=0 分支，产生方位角均匀的环向电流，其径向分布决定轴向磁场 B_z(z)。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了按扇区分解的组织原则，可用于分类与调控结构光驱动的、具有定制绕数结构的直流电流及其伴随的轴向磁场。

Abstract: Structured light can generate electronic dc currents with azimuthal winding, yet the rules governing their winding order $m$ remain unclear. Here we identify sector-resolved winding selection rules using graphene as a clean two-dimensional platform. By decomposing the current response into local and gradient sectors at the current-operator level, we show that the winding order is determined not by the optical orbital angular momentum $\ell$ alone, but by the angular structure of the corresponding current operators together with projection onto the azimuthal direction.
