For scalar Laguerre-Gaussian beams, linear polarization yields $m=|\ell\pm1|$ in the local sector and $m=|\ell|,|\ell\pm2|$ in the gradient sector, whereas circular polarization with helicity $σ=\pm1$ selects $m=|\ell-σ|$ in the local sector and $m=|\ell+2σ|$ in the gradient sector. Numerical time-evolution calculations verify these rules and further show that helicity can select an $m=0$ branch, producing an azimuthally uniform circulating current whose radial profile determines the axial magnetic field $B_z(z)$. Our results provide a sector-resolved organizing principle for classifying and controlling structured-light-driven dc currents with tailored winding structures.

</details>


### [206] [Parafermions in fractional Chern insulator-superconductor heterostructures: the role of spin polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01486)
*Aaron Amire*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文分析自旋-谷极化 ν=2/3 FQAH 边缘构成的沟道结构，指出配对与隧穿各自只与中性模的单一通道相容，因此畴壁承载 Z₃ parafermion（每对 3 个受保护态），并给出配对边缘相关性条件、库仑耦合相关性和约瑟夫森电流周期性的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往在分数量子霍尔-超导体结构中实现 Z₃ parafermion 的方案多基于自旋非极化的 ν=2/3 Halperin (1,1,2) 态；而扭角 MoTe₂ 与菱面体石墨烯中的 FQAH 态被认为是自旋-谷极化的 Jain 态，具有相同拓扑序但不同自旋结构。作者希望厘清这种自旋结构差异对边缘配对、隧穿通道选择及 parafermion 是否仍存在的问题。

Method: 分析两条极化 ν=2/3 边缘之间的沟道（trench）。在边缘模电子的配对比线性（bilinear）与隧穿双线性中筛选零共形自旋项：配对仅与中性模超导通道相容，隧穿仅与中性模背散射相容。进一步比较两通道所凝聚的中性任意子（至多差一个局域算符）、给出中性能隙形成前后的相关维数判据（边缘间耦合的 marginal/relevant 条件），并考察超导体所需配对对称性。

Result: 每个类型恰有两项零共形自旋双线性：配对只能进入中性模超导通道，隧穿只能进入中性模背散射；而非极化态中单重态配对与隧穿选择同一通道。两通道凝聚同一中性任意子（至多差局域算符），故畴壁承载 Z₃ parafermion，每对 3 个受保护态，且固定费米子宇称不增加该计数，中性通道在区域间是否改变也不影响。中性能隙形成前，排斥性边缘间耦合下配对至多 marginal，除非吸引性中性耦合偏好其通道；能隙形成后，当边缘间库仑耦合低于边缘内耦合的 7/25 时两个电荷项均 relevant。

Conclusion: 超导体须提供偶频、等自旋、谷内配对：仅含 Ising 自旋轨道耦合的 s 波单重态超导体在领头阶无法提供，而手性 p+ip 与 f+if 态可以。Z₃ 标签由 FQAH 态的准粒子携带，因此任何局限于隧穿段非 FQAH 的结区内的过程都不能改变它；预期无论是否固定费米子宇称，都存在 6π 周期约瑟夫森电流。

Abstract: Most proposals for $Z_3$ parafermions in fractional quantum Hall-superconductor structures used the spin-unpolarized $ν= 2/3$ Halperin $(1,1,2)$ state. The fractional quantum anomalous Hall (FQAH) states of twisted MoTe$_2$ and rhombohedral graphene are believed to be spin- and valley-polarized Jain states, with the same topological order but a different spin structure. We analyze a trench between two polarized $ν= 2/3$ edges. Among the pairing and tunneling bilinears of the edge-mode electrons, exactly two of each have zero conformal spin. Pairing is compatible only with the neutral-mode superconductivity channel and tunneling only with neutral-mode backscattering, whereas in the unpolarized state singlet pairing and tunneling select the same channel. Since the two channels condense the same neutral anyon up to a local operator, the domain walls carry $Z_3$ parafermions, with 3 protected states per pair, whether or not the neutral channel changes between regions; fixing the fermion parity does not enlarge this count. Before the neutral gap forms, pairing is at best marginal for repulsive inter-edge coupling unless an attractive neutral coupling favors its channel; once the gap has formed, both charge terms are relevant for an inter-edge Coulomb coupling below 7/25 of the intra-edge one. The superconductor must supply even-frequency, equal-spin, intravalley pairing, which an s-wave singlet superconductor with only Ising spin-orbit coupling does not provide at leading order, whereas chiral $p+ip$ and $f+if$ states do. The $Z_3$ label is carried by a quasiparticle of the FQAH state, so no process confined to a junction whose tunneling segments are not FQAH can change it, and we expect a $6π$-periodic Josephson current with or without fixed fermion parity.

</details>


### [207] [Single-Pulse Optical Switching combined with Current-Induced Motion of Skyrmionic Spin Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01568)
*Edoardo Mangini,Duc Minh Tran,Boonthum Kunyangyuen,Jun Xiao Lin,Raphael Gruber,Michel Hehn,Stéphane Mangin,Mathias Kläui*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在Pt/Ir/CoB/Gd/Pt多层膜中，通过单发飞秒激光脉冲实现磁斯格明子自旋结构的确定性全光翻转，并与电流驱动运动相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁斯格明子作为纳米信息载体，其位置可由电流操控，但若还能确定性调控其自旋结构，则可作为多态比特，扩展斯格明子器件的功能。作者希望将全光螺旋度无关磁化翻转（AO-HIS）与磁斯格明子耦合，实现对斯格明子内部状态的确定性光控制。

Method: 设计并制备了可稳定磁斯格明子且支持AO-HIS的Pt/Ir/CoB/Gd/Pt多层膜。用激光激发写入斯格明子织构，其极性由小幅面外磁场选择；再用单发30 fs激光脉冲在不施加磁场或电流脉冲的条件下翻转含斯格明子织构区域的磁化，从而反转其极性。随后将材料图形化为线状结构，以结合光操控与电流驱动斯格明子运动。

Result: 实现了单发30 fs激光脉冲对斯格明子极性的确定性翻转，无需磁场或电流脉冲；该光翻转区别于此前报道的光成核与湮灭。在图形化的线结构中，实现了光操控与电流驱动斯格明子运动的结合。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了斯格明子自旋结构的确定性光开关作为一种新的操控通道，提出了光编程斯格明子内部状态、电流控制其位置的光自旋电子斯格明子器件路线。

Abstract: Magnetic skyrmions are promising nanoscale information carriers because their position can be manipulated by electrical currents. The additional ability of deterministic control of the spin structure would allow the use of magnetic skyrmions as multi-state bits, expanding the capabilities of skyrmion devices further. Such control could be realized by coupling all-optical helicity independent switching of magnetization (AO-HIS) to magnetic skyrmions. This is realized in a Pt/Ir/CoB/Gd/Pt multilayer that is engineered to stabilize magnetic skyrmions and, at the same time, enable AO-HIS by single ultrafast laser pulse. Laser excitation is used to write skyrmionic textures, whose polarity is selected by a small applied out-of-plane field. A single 30 fs laser pulse is then used to toggle the magnetization of an illuminated region containing skyrmionic textures, reversing their polarities without requiring any magnetic field or current pulses. This establishes deterministic optical switching of the skyrmion spin structure as a new manipulation channel, distinct from previously reported optical nucleation and annihilation. The material is then patterned into a wire, to combine the optical manipulation with current-induced skyrmion motion. These results establish a route toward opto-spintronic skyrmion devices in which light programs the internal state of a skyrmion and electrical currents control its position.

</details>


### [208] [Flux- and tunnel-parity-controlled Josephson nonreciprocity from singlet-triplet competition in a parallel double quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01830)
*Debika Debnath,Fernando Dominguez,Patrik Recher*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了强库仑相互作用下的平行双量子点约瑟夫森结中非互易超电流的产生机制，揭示了轨道磁通与规范不变隧穿符号奇偶性对局域/非局域库珀对输运干涉的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易超电流可源于正负临界电流探测相位依赖多体谱不等价部分。作者旨在探究平行双量子点约瑟夫森结中该机制的具体表现，明确局域与非局域库珀对转移共存时的调控因素。

Method: 采用含显式超导轨道的零带宽哈密顿量进行精确对角化，在强内点库仑排斥条件下，分析轨道磁通和隧穿符号奇偶性对局域约瑟夫森过程与非局域交换过程干涉的影响，并进一步研究失谐、温度等效应。

Result: 失谐情形下出现磁通可调的单重态、三重态和双重态基态谱支及两个整流区间；在(1,1)电荷区边界，双重态支与单重态或三重态支竞争，二极管效率接近40%；三重态主导区中近邻单重态交叉非对称重塑基态包络，响应约10-15%；有限温度通常抑制电荷交叉整流，但中间温度下三重态区归一化效率与绝对临界电流非对称性均增强。

Conclusion: 结果确立了相位依赖多体支竞争作为约瑟夫森非互易性的来源，且该效应对栅压、磁通和隧穿奇偶性敏感。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal supercurrents in interacting nanostructures can arise when the positive and negative critical currents probe inequivalent portions of a phase-dependent many-body spectrum. We study this mechanism in a parallel double-quantum-dot Josephson junction with strong intradot Coulomb repulsion, where local and non-local Cooper-pair transfer coexist. Using exact diagonalization of a zero-bandwidth Hamiltonian with explicit superconducting orbitals, we show that orbital flux and the gauge-invariant tunnel-sign parity control the interference between local Josephson processes and non-local exchange. For detuned dots, this produces flux-tunable singlet, triplet, and doublet ground-state spectral branches and two rectification regimes. Near the boundaries of the charge sector with singly occupied dots [(1,1) sector], doublet branches compete with singlet or triplet branches, yielding diode efficiencies approaching 40%. Within the triplet-dominated (1,1) sector, nearby singlet crossings asymmetrically reshape the ground-state envelope even when both critical-current extrema lie on the triplet-like branch, producing a broader response of order 10-15%. Finite temperature generally suppresses charge-crossover rectification, however, for intermediate temperatures we find an enhancement of both the normalized efficiency and the absolute critical-current asymmetry in the triplet regime. These results establish phase-dependent many-body branch competition as a gate-, flux-, and tunnel-parity-sensitive source of Josephson nonreciprocity.

</details>


### [209] [Symmetry considerations in chirality-induced spin selectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01880)
*Dmitry Budker,Angela Wittmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该展望文章从基础粒子物理中常用的对称性分析出发，论证手性诱导自旋选择性（CISS）效应不违反宇称和时间反演等基本对称性，为理解CISS提供统一的对称性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CISS效应（结构手性与电子自旋极化之间的耦合）已在众多体系中实验观测到，但尽管有大量理论研究，仍缺乏统一的机理理解。作者希望借助对称性这一普适语言，为CISS的基本性质提供稳健的概念基础。

Method: 采用基础粒子物理中常用的简单对称性考虑（symmetry considerations），分析CISS效应的基本性质，特别检验其与宇称（parity）和时间反演（time-reversal）对称性的关系。

Result: 研究表明，CISS效应不违反任何基本对称性，包括宇称和时间反演对称性；通过将CISS纳入对称性框架，可以解释其若干基本特性。

Conclusion: 将CISS锚定在普适的对称性语言中，为解释实验现象、指导未来理论与实验设计提供了稳健的概念基础。

Abstract: The chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS) effect, the coupling between structural chirality and electron spin polarization, has been experimentally observed across many diverse systems. However, despite extensive theoretical effort, a unified mechanistic understanding remains elusive. In this perspective, we demonstrate how some of the basic properties of the fascinating effects of CISS can be understood based on straightforward symmetry considerations commonly employed in fundamental particle physics. In particular, we show that CISS does not violate any fundamental symmetries including parity and time-reversal. By anchoring CISS within the universal language of symmetry, we offer a robust conceptual foundation for interpreting experiments and guiding future theoretical and experimental designs.

</details>
